
===== src/proxy.ts =====
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from "next/server";

import { ROLE_HOME, canAccessPath } from "@/config/roles";

import { decrypt } from "@/app/lib/session";

/**
 * Request proxy. In Next.js 16 this file replaces `middleware.ts` —
 * the convention was renamed and the export must be `proxy`.
 *
 * OPTIMISTIC gate only: keeps users out of the wrong namespace and off login
 * screens when already signed in. Trivially bypassable, so every page
 * re-checks through the DAL and every Server Action re-authorizes on its own.
 * Server Functions POST to the route they live on, so a matcher change here
 * can silently drop coverage — never rely on this alone.
 */

/** Reachable without a session — one per principal type, plus recovery. */
const PUBLIC_PATHS = [
  "/login",
  "/employee-login",
  "/agent-login",
  "/forgot-password",
  "/reset-password",
];

/** Reachable while `must_change_password` is set, and nothing else. */
const PASSWORD_ROTATION_PATH = "/set-password";

function isPublic(pathname: string): boolean {
  return PUBLIC_PATHS.some((p) => pathname === p || pathname.startsWith(`${p}/`));
}

export async function proxy(request: NextRequest) {
  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;

  // Biometric terminals authenticate by device token, not a user session.
  if (pathname.startsWith("/kiosk")) {
    return NextResponse.next();
  }

  const session = await decrypt(request.cookies.get("dhwarsh_session")?.value);

  if (!session) {
    if (isPublic(pathname)) {
      return NextResponse.next();
    }

    // Staff login is the default door; the footer links cover the others.
    const loginUrl = new URL("/login", request.url);
    if (pathname !== "/") {
      loginUrl.searchParams.set("next", pathname);
    }
    return NextResponse.redirect(loginUrl);
  }

  const home = ROLE_HOME[session.role];

  // Pin a flagged session to the rotation screen until it completes.
  if (session.mustChangePassword) {
    return pathname === PASSWORD_ROTATION_PATH
      ? NextResponse.next()
      : NextResponse.redirect(new URL(PASSWORD_ROTATION_PATH, request.url));
  }

  // Already signed in — no reason to sit on a login screen.
  if (isPublic(pathname) || pathname === "/" || pathname === PASSWORD_ROTATION_PATH) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL(home, request.url));
  }

  // Wrong namespace: an EMPLOYEE poking at /payroll/runs lands back home.
  if (!canAccessPath(session.role, pathname)) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL(home, request.url));
  }

  return NextResponse.next();
}

export const config = {
  // Excludes static assets, image optimization and public files, so auth
  // redirects never swallow CSS, JS or images.
  matcher: [
    "/((?!api|_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico|.*\\.(?:svg|png|jpg|jpeg|gif|webp|ico)$).*)",
  ],
};

===== src/app/(portal)/layout.tsx =====
import { cookies } from "next/headers";

import { AppRail } from "@/components/layout/app-rail";
import { NavProvider, SIDEBAR_COOKIE } from "@/components/layout/nav-context";
import { StubActionBridge } from "@/components/layout/stub-action-bridge";
import { Topbar } from "@/components/layout/topbar";
import { getCurrentUser } from "@/app/lib/dal";

/**
 * Protected shell for all 9 role portals.
 *
 * `getCurrentUser()` calls `verifySession()`, which redirects to /login when
 * there is no valid session and to /set-password when
 * `users.must_change_password` is set. This is the real gate — `proxy.ts` is
 * only an optimistic pre-filter.
 *
 * The rail is built from the session role, so a user never sees navigation for
 * a namespace they cannot enter.
 *
 * Layout: full-bleed. The shell owns the whole viewport — the rail and the
 * content column are the only two children of a single flex row, so expanding
 * the rail pushes the content instead of covering it. `min-w-0` on the content
 * column is load-bearing: without it a wide table sets the flex item's minimum
 * size and the rail animation shoves the page sideways instead of reflowing it.
 *
 * `max-w-content` caps the reading column on ultrawide displays. Full-bleed
 * means the chrome reaches the edges; it does not mean a 3440px line length.
 */
export default async function PortalLayout({
  children,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  const [user, cookieStore] = await Promise.all([getCurrentUser(), cookies()]);

  // Picked on the server so the first paint is already the right width — a
  // client-side read would flash the collapsed rail on every navigation.
  const expanded = cookieStore.get(SIDEBAR_COOKIE)?.value === "1";

  return (
    <NavProvider defaultExpanded={expanded}>
      <StubActionBridge />
      <div className="flex h-dvh w-full overflow-hidden bg-surface-bright">
        {/* Chrome is interactive by construction — exempt from the stub net. */}
        <div data-live-region className="contents">
          <AppRail role={user.role} />
        </div>

        <main className="relative z-0 flex h-full min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col overflow-hidden">
          <Topbar user={user} />

          <div className="no-scrollbar flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 pb-8 pt-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-container-margin">
            <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-content">{children}</div>
          </div>
        </main>
      </div>
    </NavProvider>
  );
}

===== src/app/(portal)/admin/attendance/page.tsx =====
import Link from "next/link";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";

import { ApiError } from "@/app/lib/api-error";
import { requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";
import { AttendanceFilters } from "@/components/features/attendance/attendance-filters";
import { LiveAttendanceBoard } from "@/components/features/attendance/live-attendance-board";
import { BentoCard } from "@/components/shared/bento-card";
import { FilterTabs, type FilterTab } from "@/components/shared/filter-tabs";
import { CountPill, PageHeader } from "@/components/shared/page-header";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/shared/status-badge";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import {
  getAttendanceActivity,
  getAttendanceDevices,
  getAttendanceRecords,
  getAttendanceSummary,
  getAttendanceSyncFailures,
} from "@/lib/api/attendance";
import {
  formatDate,
  formatHours,
  formatNumber,
  formatPercent,
  formatRelativeTime,
  formatTime,
  initials,
} from "@/lib/format";
import type {
  AttendanceActivityItem,
  AttendanceDashboardSummary,
  AttendanceDeviceItem,
  AttendanceRecordItem,
  AttendanceSource,
  AttendanceSyncFailure,
  Paginated,
} from "@/types";

/**
 * Company-wide attendance board for SUPER_ADMIN and ADMIN.
 *
 * This is the screen the biometric chain was built for: an employee
 * fingerprints at a branch terminal, HrpayrollDesktop pushes the event to
 * `POST /attendance/biometric/sync`, and it surfaces here — the tiles and the
 * activity feed live (see `LiveAttendanceBoard`), the records table and the
 * device fleet on the server-rendered pass.
 *
 * Read-only by design. Corrections, manual entries and device enrolment belong
 * on the HR surfaces; an admin console that silently wrote attendance rows
 * would be a second source of truth for the same table.
 *
 * Every figure comes from `controller/attendance/AttendanceDashboardController`.
 * Nothing here is placeholder data.
 */

/** Mirrors the icon map on the HR attendance screen. */
const SOURCE_ICON: Record<AttendanceSource, string> = {
  BIOMETRIC: "fingerprint",
  MANUAL: "badge",
  MOBILE: "smartphone",
  WEB: "language",
};

/**
 * Only two tabs, because the backend exposes one boolean: `openOnly`.
 * A "Completed" tab would have to filter the current page in the browser, which
 * lies as soon as the results are paginated.
 */
const RECORD_TABS: readonly FilterTab[] = [
  { label: "All records" },
  { label: "On site now", value: "open" },
];

const RECORD_PAGE_SIZE = 25;

function first(value: string | string[] | undefined): string | undefined {
  return Array.isArray(value) ? value[0] : value;
}

function parseBranchId(value: string | undefined): number | undefined {
  if (!value) return undefined;
  const parsed = Number(value);
  return Number.isInteger(parsed) && parsed > 0 ? parsed : undefined;
}

/** `yyyy-MM-dd` only. The backend rejects anything else with a 400. */
function parseDate(value: string | undefined): string | undefined {
  return value && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value) ? value : undefined;
}

function parsePage(value: string | undefined): number {
  const parsed = Number(value);
  return Number.isInteger(parsed) && parsed > 0 ? parsed : 0;
}

/** Rebuilds the query string for a records-table page link. */
function pageHref(
  page: number,
  filters: { branchId?: number; date?: string; filter?: string },
): string {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  if (filters.branchId) params.set("branchId", String(filters.branchId));
  if (filters.date) params.set("date", filters.date);
  if (filters.filter) params.set("filter", filters.filter);
  if (page > 0) params.set("page", String(page));

  const query = params.toString();
  return query ? `/admin/attendance?${query}` : "/admin/attendance";
}

type BoardData = {
  summary: AttendanceDashboardSummary;
  activity: AttendanceActivityItem[];
  records: Paginated<AttendanceRecordItem>;
  devices: AttendanceDeviceItem[];
  syncFailures: AttendanceSyncFailure[];
};

/**
 * All five reads in one round trip.
 *
 * `sync-failures` is restricted to ADMIN_ROLES on the backend while the other
 * four are open to VIEW_ROLES — which is the same set here, because this route
 * only ever renders for SUPER_ADMIN and ADMIN. If that ever diverges, this is
 * the call that starts throwing 403 and takes the whole board down with it.
 */
async function loadBoard(filters: {
  date?: string;
  branchId?: number;
  openOnly: boolean;
  page: number;
}): Promise<BoardData> {
  const { date, branchId, openOnly, page } = filters;

  const [summary, activity, records, devices, syncFailures] = await Promise.all([
    getAttendanceSummary({ date, branchId }),
    getAttendanceActivity({ branchId, limit: 50 }),
    getAttendanceRecords({
      branchId,
      // Omitted when no date is chosen, which tells the backend "today" in the
      // company timezone rather than in the browser's.
      start: date,
      end: date,
      openOnly,
      page,
      size: RECORD_PAGE_SIZE,
    }),
    getAttendanceDevices({ branchId }),
    getAttendanceSyncFailures(),
  ]);

  return { summary, activity, records, devices, syncFailures };
}

export default async function AdminAttendancePage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{
    branchId?: string;
    date?: string;
    filter?: string;
    page?: string;
  }>;
}) {
  // SUPER_ADMIN and ADMIN both hold `attendance:read`; anyone else is bounced
  // to their own namespace before a single figure renders.
  await requirePermission("attendance:read");

  // searchParams is a Promise in Next.js 16 — it must be awaited.
  const params = await searchParams;

  const branchId = parseBranchId(first(params.branchId));
  const date = parseDate(first(params.date));
  const filter = first(params.filter);
  const openOnly = filter === "open";
  const page = parsePage(first(params.page));

  let data: BoardData;

  try {
    data = await loadBoard({ date, branchId, openOnly, page });
  } catch (error) {
    // A spent session is a session, not an error page. `api-client` has already
    // tried the refresh once, so a 401 here means the refresh token is gone too.
    if (error instanceof ApiError && error.isUnauthorized) {
      redirect("/login");
    }

    // Anything else — the backend being down is the common case — keeps the
    // page chrome and says what actually went wrong. The portal error boundary
    // deliberately hides `message`, which is the wrong trade for a dev-stage
    // service that is simply not running.
    const message =
      error instanceof ApiError
        ? error.message
        : "Unable to reach the attendance service.";

    return <LoadFailure message={message} />;
  }

  const { summary, activity, records, devices, syncFailures } = data;
  // Always page 1: "full log" means "start from the top", not "reopen whichever
  // page the records table happens to be on".
  const recordsHref = pageHref(0, { branchId, date });
  const activeRate = summary.attendanceRatePercent;

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title="Attendance"
        description="Live biometric clock-in and clock-out activity across every branch."
        pill={
          <CountPill
            label={`${formatPercent(activeRate)} reported`}
            tone={
              activeRate >= 85 ? "success" : activeRate > 0 ? "warning" : "neutral"
            }
          />
        }
        actions={
          <AttendanceFilters
            branches={summary.branches}
            branchId={branchId}
            date={date ?? summary.date}
            filter={filter}
          />
        }
      />

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-gutter">
        <LiveAttendanceBoard
          initialSnapshot={{
            summary,
            activity,
            fetchedAt: summary.generatedAt,
          }}
          branchId={branchId}
          recordsHref={recordsHref}
        />

        <div className="grid grid-cols-12 gap-gutter">
          {/* ───────────────────────────── main column ───────────────────── */}
          <div className="col-span-12 flex flex-col gap-gutter lg:col-span-8">
            <BentoCard
              title={
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Icon name="table_view" className="text-[18px] text-on-surface-variant" />
                  {openOnly ? "On site now" : "Attendance records"}
                </span>
              }
              subtitle={`${formatDate(summary.date)} · ${formatNumber(records.totalElements)} ${
                records.totalElements === 1 ? "record" : "records"
              }`}
              action={
                <FilterTabs
                  tabs={RECORD_TABS}
                  active={filter}
                  basePath="/admin/attendance"
                  preserve={{
                    branchId: branchId ? String(branchId) : undefined,
                    date,
                  }}
                />
              }
            >
              {records.content.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="rounded-card border border-dashed border-outline-variant px-4 py-12 text-center">
                  <Icon
                    name="event_busy"
                    className="text-[40px] text-outline-variant"
                  />
                  <p className="mt-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant">
                    {openOnly
                      ? "Nobody is clocked in right now."
                      : "No attendance recorded for this day."}
                  </p>
                  {/* The likeliest reason on a fresh install is that the
                      terminal has nothing to report with, not that nobody
                      showed up. */}
                  <p className="mx-auto mt-1 max-w-md text-[11px] text-outline">
                    Biometric events only reach this table once the branch
                    terminal is registered under Devices and each employee has
                    enrolled a fingerprint on it.
                  </p>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                  <table className="data-table w-full border-collapse text-left">
                    <thead>
                      <tr>
                        <th className="pb-3 pl-2 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline">
                          Employee
                        </th>
                        <th className="pb-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline">
                          Branch
                        </th>
                        <th className="pb-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline">
                          Position
                        </th>
                        <th className="pb-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline">
                          Clock In
                        </th>
                        <th className="pb-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline">
                          Clock Out
                        </th>
                        <th className="pb-3 text-right font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline">
                          Hours
                        </th>
                        <th className="pb-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline">
                          Source
                        </th>
                        <th className="pb-3 pr-2 text-right font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline">
                          Status
                        </th>
                      </tr>
                    </thead>

                    <tbody className="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface">
                      {records.content.map((record) => (
                        <tr key={record.id} className="transition-colors">
                          <td className="py-3 pl-2">
                            <span className="flex items-center gap-3">
                              <span
                                aria-hidden
                                className="flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary-container text-[11px] font-bold text-on-primary"
                              >
                                {initials(record.employeeName)}
                              </span>
                              <span className="min-w-0">
                                <span className="block truncate font-medium">
                                  {record.employeeName ?? "Unknown employee"}
                                </span>
                                <span className="block text-[10px] text-outline">
                                  {record.employeeNumber ?? "—"}
                                </span>
                              </span>
                            </span>
                          </td>
                          <td className="py-3 text-on-surface-variant">
                            {record.branchName ?? "—"}
                          </td>
                          <td className="py-3 text-on-surface-variant">
                            {record.position ?? "—"}
                          </td>
                          <td className="py-3 tabular-nums">
                            {formatTime(record.clockIn)}
                          </td>
                          <td className="py-3 tabular-nums text-outline">
                            {formatTime(record.clockOut)}
                          </td>
                          <td className="py-3 text-right tabular-nums">
                            {formatHours(record.hoursWorked)}
                          </td>
                          <td className="py-3">
                            <span
                              className="flex items-center gap-1 text-outline"
                              title={record.source.toLowerCase()}
                            >
                              <Icon
                                name={SOURCE_ICON[record.source]}
                                className="text-[18px]"
                              />
                              <span className="sr-only">
                                {record.source.toLowerCase()}
                              </span>
                            </span>
                          </td>
                          <td className="py-3 pr-2 text-right">
                            <StatusBadge status={record.status} />
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>

                  {/* Pagination is links, not buttons: the page number belongs
                      in the URL like every other filter on this screen. */}
                  {records.totalPages > 1 ? (
                    <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-outline-variant/40 pt-4 text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant">
                      <span>
                        Page {formatNumber(records.pageNumber + 1)} of{" "}
                        {formatNumber(records.totalPages)}
                      </span>

                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                        {records.pageNumber > 0 ? (
                          <Link
                            href={pageHref(records.pageNumber - 1, {
                              branchId,
                              date,
                              filter,
                            })}
                            className="rounded-md px-2.5 py-1 transition-colors hover:bg-surface-container-low"
                          >
                            Previous
                          </Link>
                        ) : null}

                        {!records.last ? (
                          <Link
                            href={pageHref(records.pageNumber + 1, {
                              branchId,
                              date,
                              filter,
                            })}
                            className="rounded-md px-2.5 py-1 transition-colors hover:bg-surface-container-low"
                          >
                            Next
                          </Link>
                        ) : null}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : null}
                </div>
              )}
            </BentoCard>
          </div>

          {/* ──────────────────────────── side column ────────────────────── */}
          <div className="col-span-12 flex flex-col gap-gutter lg:col-span-4">
            <BentoCard
              title={
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Icon name="dns" className="text-[18px] text-on-surface-variant" />
                  Terminals
                </span>
              }
              subtitle={`${formatNumber(summary.devicesOnline)} of ${formatNumber(summary.devicesTotal)} reporting`}
            >
              {devices.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="py-6 text-center font-body-md text-body-md text-outline">
                  No terminals registered for this branch yet.
                </p>
              ) : (
                <ul className="flex flex-col gap-3">
                  {devices.map((device) => (
                    <li
                      key={device.id}
                      className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3"
                    >
                      <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2.5">
                        <span
                          aria-hidden
                          className={`size-2 shrink-0 rounded-full ${
                            device.online ? "bg-green-main" : "bg-outline-variant"
                          }`}
                        />
                        <span className="min-w-0">
                          <span className="block truncate font-mono text-[11px] text-on-surface">
                            {device.deviceSerial}
                          </span>
                          <span className="block truncate text-[10px] text-outline">
                            {device.branchName ?? "Unassigned"}
                          </span>
                        </span>
                      </span>

                      <span className="shrink-0 text-right text-[10px] text-on-surface-variant">
                        {device.online ? (
                          <span className="font-semibold text-green-main">
                            Online
                          </span>
                        ) : (
                          formatRelativeTime(device.lastSyncAt)
                        )}
                      </span>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              )}
            </BentoCard>

            <BentoCard
              title={
                <span
                  className={
                    syncFailures.length > 0
                      ? "flex items-center gap-2 text-error"
                      : "flex items-center gap-2 text-on-surface"
                  }
                >
                  <Icon name="sync_problem" className="text-[18px]" />
                  Rejected Scans
                </span>
              }
              subtitle="Scans the backend refused"
            >
              {syncFailures.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="py-6 text-center font-body-md text-body-md text-outline">
                  No rejected scans.
                </p>
              ) : (
                <ul className="flex flex-col gap-3">
                  {syncFailures.slice(0, 8).map((failure) => (
                    <li
                      key={failure.id}
                      className="rounded-lg border border-error/20 bg-error/5 p-3"
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                        <span className="min-w-0 truncate text-[11px] font-semibold text-on-surface">
                          {failure.employeeName ??
                            failure.deviceSerial ??
                            "Unknown terminal"}
                        </span>
                        <span className="shrink-0 text-[10px] text-outline">
                          {formatRelativeTime(failure.createdAt)}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-error">
                        {failure.reason}
                      </p>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              )}

              {syncFailures.length > 0 ? (
                <p className="mt-3 text-[10px] text-outline">
                  A rejected scan leaves no attendance row. Reprovision the
                  employee on the terminal, then have them scan again.
                </p>
              ) : null}
            </BentoCard>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

/**
 * Shown when the API cannot be reached at all.
 *
 * Deliberately keeps the page header's slot empty rather than rendering a
 * half-populated board: a dashboard that shows zeros because the backend is
 * down reads exactly like a dashboard that shows zeros because nobody came to
 * work, and an administrator will act on the second reading.
 */
function LoadFailure({ message }: { message: string }) {
  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title="Attendance"
        description="Live biometric clock-in and clock-out activity across every branch."
      />

      <div className="rounded-card border border-dashed border-error/40 bg-error/5 p-8 text-center">
        <Icon name="cloud_off" className="text-[40px] text-error" />
        <p className="mt-3 font-body-md text-body-md font-semibold text-on-surface">
          Attendance could not be loaded
        </p>
        <p className="mx-auto mt-1 max-w-md font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant">
          {message}
        </p>
        <p className="mx-auto mt-3 max-w-md text-[11px] text-outline">
          Check that the backend is running and that <code>API_BASE_URL</code> in{" "}
          <code>.env.local</code> matches the port it listens on.
        </p>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

===== src/app/(portal)/admin/dashboard/page.tsx =====
import Link from "next/link";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";

import { ApiError } from "@/app/lib/api-error";
import { requireAnyPermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";
import { AlertSpotlight } from "@/components/shared/alert-spotlight";
import { BentoCard } from "@/components/shared/bento-card";
import { DataUnavailable } from "@/components/shared/data-unavailable";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/shared/empty-state";
import { SegmentStrip } from "@/components/shared/segment-strip";
import { StatCard } from "@/components/shared/stat-card";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { getAgents } from "@/lib/api/agent";
import { getAttendanceDevices, getAttendanceSummary } from "@/lib/api/attendance";
import { getBranches } from "@/lib/api/branch";
import { getEmployeesByBranch } from "@/lib/api/employee";
import { formatNumber, formatPercent, formatRelativeTime } from "@/lib/format";
import type { AttendanceDeviceItem, BranchResponse } from "@/types";

/**
 * Admin dashboard. Serves SUPER_ADMIN and ADMIN — both own /admin.
 *
 * Everything here is a real read: branch directory, the attendance summary for
 * today, the agent list, the terminal fleet, and a per-branch employee count.
 *
 * Five widgets the placeholder carried are not here, because nothing in the
 * schema or the API can answer them:
 *
 *   · **Headcount Growth** — a seven-month series. Nothing records headcount
 *     over time; `employees` holds only the current row, so a trend line would
 *     have to be invented wholesale.
 *   · **Payroll Status** (disbursed / processed / pending on hold) — needs the
 *     payroll-run list, which the backend does not expose yet.
 *   · **Awaiting Approval** — the approval queues are scoped per role
 *     (`/leave/pending/manager` and friends); there is no company-wide pending
 *     queue to render for an administrator.
 *   · **Workforce Split** — the donut needs `employment_type`, which the
 *     `by-branch` list does not return. Only the single-employee read carries
 *     it, so a company-wide split would be one request per employee.
 *   · **Payroll KES** per branch — no per-branch payroll total exists.
 *
 * Each returns with the aggregate endpoint that supplies it. Until then an
 * empty slot is honest and a fabricated one is not — the figures above were
 * realistic enough to be believed, which is exactly what made them dangerous on
 * a screen an administrator uses to decide things.
 */

/** A terminal that has not synced in this long reads as not reporting. */
const DEVICE_STALE_MINUTES = 60;

function isDeviceStale(device: AttendanceDeviceItem): boolean {
  if (device.status !== "ACTIVE") return false;
  if (!device.lastSyncAt) return true;

  const ageMs = Date.now() - new Date(device.lastSyncAt).getTime();
  return ageMs > DEVICE_STALE_MINUTES * 60_000;
}

export default async function AdminDashboardPage() {
  // ADMIN and SUPER_ADMIN both hold these; anyone else is bounced to their own
  // namespace before a single figure renders.
  await requireAnyPermission(["company:read", "branch:read"]);

  let branches: BranchResponse[];

  try {
    branches = await getBranches();
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ApiError && error.isUnauthorized) redirect("/login");

    return (
      <DataUnavailable
        title="Admin Dashboard"
        description="Company-wide overview across branches, staff and attendance."
        subject="The dashboard"
        reason={error instanceof ApiError ? error.message : undefined}
      />
    );
  }

  // Secondary reads are independent of each other and of the branch directory.
  // `allSettled` throughout: one unavailable panel must not blank the page, and
  // an empty slot is a truthful rendering of "could not read this".
  const [summaryResult, agentsResult, devicesResult] = await Promise.allSettled([
    getAttendanceSummary(),
    getAgents(),
    getAttendanceDevices(),
  ]);

  const countResults = await Promise.allSettled(
    branches.map((branch) => getEmployeesByBranch(branch.id)),
  );

  const summary =
    summaryResult.status === "fulfilled" ? summaryResult.value : null;
  const agents =
    agentsResult.status === "fulfilled" ? agentsResult.value : null;
  const devices =
    devicesResult.status === "fulfilled" ? devicesResult.value : null;

  const headcount = new Map<number, number | null>(
    branches.map((branch, index) => {
      const result = countResults[index];
      return [
        branch.id,
        result && result.status === "fulfilled" ? result.value.length : null,
      ];
    }),
  );

  const totalEmployees = [...headcount.values()]
    .filter((value): value is number => value !== null)
    .reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0);

  const activeBranches = branches.filter((b) => b.status === "ACTIVE").length;
  const staleDevices = (devices ?? []).filter(isDeviceStale);

  /** Attendance rate for one branch, from the summary's per-branch roll-up. */
  const rateFor = (branchId: number): number | null => {
    const row = summary?.branches.find((b) => b.branchId === branchId);
    return row ? row.attendanceRatePercent : null;
  };

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-gutter md:grid-cols-12">
      {/* ───────────────────────────────── left column ─────────────────── */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-gutter md:col-span-8">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-gutter sm:grid-cols-3">
          <StatCard
            label="Total Employees"
            value={formatNumber(totalEmployees)}
            icon="group"
            badgeIcon="badge"
          />
          <StatCard
            label="Active Branches"
            value={formatNumber(activeBranches)}
            icon="corporate_fare"
            badgeIcon="storefront"
          />
          <StatCard
            label="Labour Agents"
            value={agents === null ? "—" : formatNumber(agents.length)}
            icon="engineering"
            badgeIcon="handshake"
          />
        </div>

        <BentoCard
          title="Branch Performance"
          subtitle="Headcount and today's attendance rate, per branch"
          action={
            <Link
              href="/admin/branches"
              className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded border border-outline-variant/40 px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:bg-surface-container"
            >
              <Icon name="arrow_forward" className="text-sm" />
              All Branches
            </Link>
          }
        >
          {branches.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState
              symbol="location_off"
              title="No branches yet"
              description="Branches appear here once they have been created."
            />
          ) : (
            <div className="w-full overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="data-table w-full border-collapse text-left">
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th className="pb-3 pl-2 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline">
                      Branch
                    </th>
                    <th className="pb-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline">
                      Manager
                    </th>
                    <th className="pb-3 text-right font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline">
                      Employees
                    </th>
                    <th className="pb-3 pr-2 text-right font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline">
                      Attendance&nbsp;%
                    </th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>

                <tbody className="font-body-md text-body-md">
                  {branches.map((branch) => {
                    const count = headcount.get(branch.id);
                    const rate = rateFor(branch.id);

                    return (
                      <tr key={branch.id} className="group transition-colors">
                        <td className="py-3 pl-2">
                          <Link
                            href={`/admin/branches/${branch.id}`}
                            className="flex items-center gap-2"
                          >
                            <span className="flex size-6 items-center justify-center rounded bg-primary-container/10 text-primary-container">
                              <Icon name="location_city" className="text-sm" />
                            </span>
                            <span className="font-medium text-on-surface group-hover:text-primary-container">
                              {branch.name}
                            </span>
                          </Link>
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 text-on-surface-variant">
                          {branch.managerName ?? (
                            <span className="text-amber-main">Unassigned</span>
                          )}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 text-right tabular-nums text-on-surface-variant">
                          {count === null || count === undefined
                            ? "—"
                            : formatNumber(count)}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 pr-2 text-right tabular-nums text-on-surface-variant">
                          {rate === null ? "—" : formatPercent(rate)}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}
        </BentoCard>
      </div>

      {/* ──────────────────────────────── right column ─────────────────── */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-gutter md:col-span-4">
        {summary ? (
          <SegmentStrip
            segments={[
              { label: "Expected", value: formatNumber(summary.expectedEmployees) },
              { label: "Reported", value: formatNumber(summary.reported) },
              {
                label: "Not Yet In",
                value: formatNumber(summary.notYetIn),
                tone: summary.notYetIn > 0 ? "warning" : "default",
              },
            ]}
          />
        ) : null}

        {staleDevices.length > 0 ? (
          <AlertSpotlight
            message={`${formatNumber(staleDevices.length)} ${
              staleDevices.length === 1 ? "terminal has" : "terminals have"
            } not synced in the last hour.`}
            href="/admin/devices"
          />
        ) : null}

        <BentoCard title="Attendance Today">
          {summary ? (
            <>
              <p className="font-card-stat text-card-stat tabular-nums text-on-surface">
                {formatPercent(summary.attendanceRatePercent, 1)}
              </p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-[10px] text-outline">
                {formatNumber(summary.reported)} of{" "}
                {formatNumber(summary.expectedEmployees)} employees reported
              </p>
              <div className="mt-4 border-t border-outline-variant/40 pt-3 text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant">
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span>Currently in</span>
                  <span className="tabular-nums">
                    {formatNumber(summary.currentlyIn)}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="mt-1.5 flex justify-between">
                  <span>Clocked out</span>
                  <span className="tabular-nums">
                    {formatNumber(summary.clockedOut)}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <Link
                href="/admin/attendance"
                className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-primary-container hover:underline"
              >
                Attendance board
                <Icon name="arrow_forward" className="text-sm" />
              </Link>
            </>
          ) : (
            <p className="py-4 text-center font-body-md text-body-md text-outline">
              Attendance for today could not be read.
            </p>
          )}
        </BentoCard>

        <BentoCard title="Device Fleet">
          {devices ? (
            <>
              <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
                <span className="font-card-stat text-card-stat tabular-nums text-on-surface">
                  {formatNumber(summary?.devicesOnline ?? 0)}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant">
                  of {formatNumber(devices.length)} reporting
                </span>
              </div>

              {staleDevices.length > 0 ? (
                <ul className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-2 border-t border-outline-variant/40 pt-3">
                  {staleDevices.slice(0, 4).map((device) => (
                    <li
                      key={device.id}
                      className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-[11px]"
                    >
                      <span className="min-w-0 truncate font-mono text-on-surface-variant">
                        {device.deviceSerial}
                      </span>
                      <span className="shrink-0 text-error">
                        {device.lastSyncAt
                          ? formatRelativeTime(device.lastSyncAt)
                          : "never"}
                      </span>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              ) : (
                <p className="mt-3 text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant">
                  Every active terminal has synced recently.
                </p>
              )}

              <Link
                href="/admin/devices"
                className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-primary-container hover:underline"
              >
                Manage devices
                <Icon name="arrow_forward" className="text-sm" />
              </Link>
            </>
          ) : (
            <p className="py-4 text-center font-body-md text-body-md text-outline">
              The device fleet could not be read.
            </p>
          )}
        </BentoCard>

        <BentoCard title="Labour Agents">
          <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
            <span className="font-card-stat text-card-stat tabular-nums text-on-surface">
              {agents === null ? "—" : formatNumber(agents.length)}
            </span>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant">
              registered suppliers
            </span>
          </div>
          {agents && agents.length > 0 ? (
            <p className="mt-3 text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant">
              {formatNumber(
                agents.reduce((sum, a) => sum + (a.employeeCount ?? 0), 0),
              )}{" "}
              staff deployed across them.
            </p>
          ) : null}
          <Link
            href="/admin/agents"
            className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-primary-container hover:underline"
          >
            Agent directory
            <Icon name="arrow_forward" className="text-sm" />
          </Link>
        </BentoCard>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/app/(portal)/hr/employees/page.tsx =====
import Link from "next/link";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import { ApiError } from "@/app/lib/api-error";
import { requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";
import { BentoCard } from "@/components/shared/bento-card";
import { DataUnavailable } from "@/components/shared/data-unavailable";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/shared/empty-state";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/shared/page-header";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/shared/status-badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { buttonVariants } from "@/components/ui/button-variants";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { getBranches } from "@/lib/api/branch";
import { getEmployeesByBranch } from "@/lib/api/employee";
import { formatNumber } from "@/lib/format";
import type { BranchResponse, EmployeeSummaryResponse } from "@/types";

/**
 * Employee directory.
 *
 * There is **no `GET /employees`** on the backend, so this is assembled branch
 * by branch — one read per branch, concurrently. That is a real shape of the
 * API rather than a shortcut here, and it has two consequences worth knowing:
 *
 *  - The table cannot be sorted or paginated server-side, because it is not one
 *    result set. Filtering happens after the merge, over the whole company.
 *  - The rows are `EmployeeSummaryResponse`, which is deliberately thin: name,
 *    number, branch, position, status. No salary and no statutory identifiers
 *    appear in any list — only `GET /employees/{id}` returns those.
 *
 * A branch whose read fails contributes no rows rather than failing the page,
 * so one bad branch does not hide every other branch's staff.
 */
export default async function EmployeesPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ branch?: string; q?: string }>;
}) {
  await requirePermission("employee:read");

  const { branch: branchFilter, q } = await searchParams;

  let branches: BranchResponse[];

  try {
    branches = await getBranches();
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ApiError && error.isUnauthorized) {
      throw error;
    }

    return (
      <DataUnavailable
        title="Employees"
        description="Every employee across the company's branches."
        subject="The branch list"
        reason={
          error instanceof ApiError && error.isForbidden
            ? "Your role is not permitted to read /branches yet, and the directory is assembled branch by branch. See docs/backend-patches/HR_MANAGER_READ_ACCESS.md."
            : error instanceof ApiError
              ? error.message
              : undefined
        }
      />
    );
  }

  const visibleBranches = branchFilter
    ? branches.filter((b) => String(b.id) === branchFilter)
    : branches;

  const results = await Promise.allSettled(
    visibleBranches.map((branch) => getEmployeesByBranch(branch.id)),
  );

  // An all-branches failure is a backend problem worth reporting; a single
  // branch's failure inside a working company is not.
  const everyReadFailed =
    results.length > 0 && results.every((r) => r.status === "rejected");

  if (everyReadFailed) {
    const first = results[0];

    return (
      <DataUnavailable
        title="Employees"
        description="Every employee across the company's branches."
        subject="The employee list"
        reason={
          first.status === "rejected" && first.reason instanceof ApiError
            ? first.reason.message
            : undefined
        }
      />
    );
  }

  const employees: EmployeeSummaryResponse[] = results
    .flatMap((result) => (result.status === "fulfilled" ? result.value : []))
    .sort((a, b) => a.fullName.localeCompare(b.fullName));

  const failedBranchCount = results.filter((r) => r.status === "rejected").length;

  const search = q?.trim().toLowerCase() ?? "";
  const filtered = search
    ? employees.filter(
        (e) =>
          e.fullName.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
          e.employeeNumber.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
          (e.position ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(search),
      )
    : employees;

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title="Employees"
        description="Every employee across the company's branches."
        actions={
          <Link
            href="/hr/employees/new"
            className={buttonVariants({ className: "w-fit" })}
          >
            <Icon name="person_add" className="text-[18px]" />
            Add Employee
          </Link>
        }
      />

      {failedBranchCount > 0 ? (
        <p
          role="status"
          className="mb-4 rounded-lg border border-amber-main/40 bg-amber-main/5 px-4 py-3 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant"
        >
          {formatNumber(failedBranchCount)}{" "}
          {failedBranchCount === 1 ? "branch" : "branches"} could not be read, so
          the total below is incomplete.
        </p>
      ) : null}

      <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <FilterLink href="/hr/employees" active={!branchFilter}>
          All branches
        </FilterLink>
        {branches.map((branch) => (
          <FilterLink
            key={branch.id}
            href={`/hr/employees?branch=${branch.id}`}
            active={branchFilter === String(branch.id)}
          >
            {branch.name}
          </FilterLink>
        ))}
      </div>

      <form className="mb-4 flex gap-2" action="/hr/employees">
        {branchFilter ? (
          <input type="hidden" name="branch" value={branchFilter} />
        ) : null}
        <input
          type="search"
          name="q"
          defaultValue={q ?? ""}
          placeholder="Search by name, number or job title"
          className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-lg border border-outline-variant bg-surface-container-lowest px-3 py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface focus:border-primary-container focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-container/20"
        />
        <Button type="submit" variant="secondary" size="md">
          Search
        </Button>
      </form>

      <BentoCard
        title="Directory"
        action={
          <span className="font-body-md text-[11px] text-outline">
            {formatNumber(filtered.length)}{" "}
            {filtered.length === 1 ? "employee" : "employees"}
          </span>
        }
      >
        {filtered.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState
            symbol="group_off"
            title={search ? "Nobody matches that search" : "No employees yet"}
            description={
              search
                ? "Try a different name, employee number or job title."
                : "Add the first employee to see them here."
            }
          />
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left font-body-md text-body-md">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-outline-variant/50 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline">
                  <th className="pb-3 pr-4">Employee</th>
                  <th className="pb-3 pr-4">Number</th>
                  <th className="pb-3 pr-4">Branch</th>
                  <th className="pb-3 pr-4">Position</th>
                  <th className="pb-3 text-right">Status</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {filtered.map((employee) => (
                  <tr
                    key={employee.id}
                    className="border-b border-outline-variant/25 last:border-0"
                  >
                    <td className="py-3 pr-4">
                      <Link
                        href={`/hr/employees/${employee.id}`}
                        className="font-semibold text-on-surface hover:text-primary"
                      >
                        {employee.fullName}
                      </Link>
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 pr-4 font-mono text-[12px] text-on-surface-variant">
                      {employee.employeeNumber}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 pr-4 text-on-surface-variant">
                      {employee.branchName ?? "—"}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 pr-4 text-on-surface-variant">
                      {employee.position ?? "—"}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 text-right">
                      <StatusBadge status={employee.employmentStatus} />
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </BentoCard>
    </>
  );
}

function FilterLink({
  href,
  active,
  children,
}: {
  href: string;
  active: boolean;
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <Link
      href={href}
      className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 font-body-md text-[11px] font-semibold transition-colors ${
        active
          ? "bg-primary text-on-primary"
          : "bg-surface-container text-on-surface-variant hover:bg-surface-container-high"
      }`}
    >
      {children}
    </Link>
  );
}

===== src/app/(portal)/hr/employees/new/page.tsx =====
import { ApiError } from "@/app/lib/api-error";
import { requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";
import { DataUnavailable } from "@/components/shared/data-unavailable";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/shared/page-header";
import { getAgents } from "@/lib/api/agent";
import { getBranches } from "@/lib/api/branch";
import { getDepartmentsByBranch } from "@/lib/api/department";
import type { AgentResponse, BranchResponse, DepartmentResponse } from "@/types";

import { EmployeeForm } from "../_components/employee-form";

/**
 * Add Employee.
 *
 * The form posts to `POST /employees` through a Server Action. Everything this
 * page reads is only there to populate the dropdowns: branches (required),
 * departments per branch (filtered by branch, and the backend rejects a
 * mismatch), and agents (optional).
 *
 * HR_MANAGER is authorised for `POST /employees` — that is not in question.
 * What it needs *as well* is read access to `/branches` and `/agents`, because
 * a create call is useless if the form cannot offer a branch to choose. Those
 * two reads are granted by `docs/backend-patches/HR_MANAGER_READ_ACCESS.md`;
 * until that is applied the branch read comes back 403 and this page says so
 * rather than rendering an empty dropdown that looks like an empty company.
 */
export default async function NewEmployeePage() {
  await requirePermission("employee:write");

  let branches: BranchResponse[];

  try {
    branches = await getBranches();
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ApiError && error.isForbidden) {
      return (
        <DataUnavailable
          title="Add Employee"
          description="Create a new employee record."
          subject="The branch list"
          reason="Your role is not permitted to read /branches yet, so there is no branch to file the employee under. This is a backend permission that has been identified and is waiting to be applied — see docs/backend-patches/HR_MANAGER_READ_ACCESS.md."
        />
      );
    }

    return (
      <DataUnavailable
        title="Add Employee"
        description="Create a new employee record."
        subject="The branch list"
        reason={error instanceof ApiError ? error.message : undefined}
      />
    );
  }

  // Agents are optional on the form (a direct employee has none) and the
  // department read is one call per branch. Both are allowed to fail without
  // blocking the page: a missing department list narrows the user's choices,
  // whereas a missing branch list makes the form impossible.
  const [agentsResult, departmentResults] = await Promise.all([
    getAgents().catch(() => [] as AgentResponse[]),
    Promise.allSettled(branches.map((branch) => getDepartmentsByBranch(branch.id))),
  ]);

  const departmentsByBranch: Record<number, DepartmentResponse[]> = {};

  branches.forEach((branch, index) => {
    const result = departmentResults[index];

    departmentsByBranch[branch.id] =
      result?.status === "fulfilled" ? result.value : [];
  });

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title="Add Employee"
        description="Create a new employee record."
      />

      <EmployeeForm
        mode="create"
        options={{ branches, departmentsByBranch, agents: agentsResult }}
      />
    </>
  );
}

===== src/app/(portal)/hr/employees/[id]/edit/page.tsx =====
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";

import { ApiError } from "@/app/lib/api-error";
import { requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";
import { DataUnavailable } from "@/components/shared/data-unavailable";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/shared/page-header";
import { getAgents } from "@/lib/api/agent";
import { getBranches } from "@/lib/api/branch";
import { getDepartmentsByBranch } from "@/lib/api/department";
import { getEmployee } from "@/lib/api/employee";
import type { AgentResponse, BranchResponse, DepartmentResponse } from "@/types";

import { EmployeeForm } from "../../_components/employee-form";

/**
 * Edit Employee.
 *
 * `PUT /employees/{id}` takes the same `EmployeeRequest` as create, so this
 * page reuses the create form rather than maintaining a second, drifting copy
 * of the field list. The one difference is the hidden `id` field.
 *
 * `employeeNumber` is shown but not editable — it is generated once at creation
 * and the request DTO has no field for it.
 */
export default async function EditEmployeePage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
}) {
  await requirePermission("employee:write");

  const { id } = await params;
  const employeeId = Number(id);

  if (!Number.isInteger(employeeId) || employeeId <= 0) {
    notFound();
  }

  let employee;
  let branches: BranchResponse[];

  try {
    [employee, branches] = await Promise.all([
      getEmployee(employeeId),
      getBranches(),
    ]);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ApiError) {
      if (error.isNotFound) notFound();

      if (error.isForbidden) {
        return (
          <DataUnavailable
            title="Edit Employee"
            description="Update employee records and compliance data."
            subject="This employee's record"
            reason="Your role is not permitted to read /branches yet, so the branch and department fields cannot be filled. See docs/backend-patches/HR_MANAGER_READ_ACCESS.md."
          />
        );
      }
    }

    throw error;
  }

  const [agentsResult, departmentResults] = await Promise.all([
    getAgents().catch(() => [] as AgentResponse[]),
    Promise.allSettled(branches.map((branch) => getDepartmentsByBranch(branch.id))),
  ]);

  const departmentsByBranch: Record<number, DepartmentResponse[]> = {};

  branches.forEach((branch, index) => {
    const result = departmentResults[index];

    departmentsByBranch[branch.id] =
      result?.status === "fulfilled" ? result.value : [];
  });

  // The employee's own department may not be in the list if their branch read
  // failed or they were moved since; the form's select falls back to "None"
  // rather than silently submitting a department from another branch.
  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title={`Edit ${employee.fullName}`}
        description={`Employee ${employee.employeeNumber} — update records and compliance data.`}
      />

      <EmployeeForm
        mode="edit"
        initial={employee}
        options={{ branches, departmentsByBranch, agents: agentsResult }}
      />
    </>
  );
}

===== src/app/(portal)/hr/employees/_components/employee-form.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useActionState, useState } from "react";

import {
  createEmployeeAction,
  updateEmployeeAction,
} from "@/app/actions/employee";
import { BentoCard } from "@/components/shared/bento-card";
import { SubmitButton } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { useActionToast } from "@/hooks/use-action-toast";
import { toDateInput } from "@/lib/format";
import type {
  ActionState,
  AgentResponse,
  BranchResponse,
  DepartmentResponse,
  EmployeeResponse,
} from "@/types";

/**
 * The employee form, used by both /hr/employees/new and /hr/employees/[id]/edit.
 *
 * Its field list is `EmployeeRequest` and nothing else. Three absences are
 * deliberate and worth naming, because each one was a bug in the previous
 * version of this screen:
 *
 *  - **No employee number.** The service mints `EMP-<branchCode>-<seq>` and
 *    retries on collision. A field here would either be ignored or fight it.
 *  - **No employment status.** It is not on the request DTO. The entity
 *    defaults to ACTIVE. Only terminate changes it, and that is its own action.
 *  - **No payment method, no profile photo.** Neither endpoint exists. A
 *    control that silently discards a value is worse than no control.
 *
 * The Branch → Department cascade is not cosmetic: the service rejects a
 * department that does not belong to the chosen branch, so the department list
 * is filtered to the selected branch and cleared when the branch changes.
 */

export type EmployeeFormOptions = {
  branches: BranchResponse[];
  /** Keyed by branch id — one read per branch, done on the server. */
  departmentsByBranch: Record<number, DepartmentResponse[]>;
  agents: AgentResponse[];
};

export function EmployeeForm({
  mode,
  options,
  initial,
}: {
  mode: "create" | "edit";
  options: EmployeeFormOptions;
  initial?: EmployeeResponse;
}) {
  // Remounting on "Add another" resets both the uncontrolled inputs and the
  // useActionState result, which cannot be reset in place.
  const [instance, setInstance] = useState(0);

  return (
    <EmployeeFormInstance
      key={instance}
      mode={mode}
      options={options}
      initial={initial}
      onStartAnother={() => setInstance((n) => n + 1)}
    />
  );
}

function EmployeeFormInstance({
  mode,
  options,
  initial,
  onStartAnother,
}: {
  mode: "create" | "edit";
  options: EmployeeFormOptions;
  initial?: EmployeeResponse;
  onStartAnother: () => void;
}) {
  const action = mode === "create" ? createEmployeeAction : updateEmployeeAction;

  const [state, formAction] = useActionState<
    ActionState<EmployeeResponse>,
    FormData
  >(action, { status: "idle" });

  useActionToast(state, {
    success:
      mode === "create"
        ? (employee) => `${employee.fullName} created as ${employee.employeeNumber}`
        : "Employee updated",
  });

  const errors = state.status === "error" ? state.fieldErrors : undefined;
  const created = state.status === "success" ? state.data : undefined;

  // Branch and department are the one controlled pair on the form: the
  // department list is a function of the branch, and the service rejects a
  // department that belongs to a different branch. Every other field is
  // uncontrolled, so a failed submit leaves the user's typing in place.
  const [branchId, setBranchId] = useState(
    initial ? String(initial.branchId) : "",
  );
  const [departmentId, setDepartmentId] = useState(
    initial?.departmentId ? String(initial.departmentId) : "",
  );

  const departments = options.departmentsByBranch[Number(branchId)] ?? [];

  if (created && mode === "create") {
    return <CreatedPanel employee={created} onStartAnother={onStartAnother} />;
  }

  return (
    <form action={formAction} className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-gutter pb-24 lg:grid-cols-12">
      {mode === "edit" && initial ? (
        <input type="hidden" name="id" value={initial.id} />
      ) : null}

      {/* ───────────────────────────────── main column ─────────────────── */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-gutter lg:col-span-8">
        {state.status === "error" ? (
          <p
            role="alert"
            className="rounded-lg border border-error/40 bg-error/5 px-4 py-3 font-body-md text-body-md text-error"
          >
            {state.message}
          </p>
        ) : null}

        <BentoCard title="Personal Details">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-gutter md:grid-cols-2">
            <Field
              label="Full Name"
              name="fullName"
              placeholder="John Doe"
              required
              defaultValue={initial?.fullName}
              error={errors?.fullName?.[0]}
            />
            <Field
              label="Date of Birth"
              name="dateOfBirth"
              type="date"
              defaultValue={toDateInput(initial?.dateOfBirth)}
              error={errors?.dateOfBirth?.[0]}
            />
            <Select
              label="Gender"
              name="gender"
              placeholder="Select Gender"
              defaultValue={initial?.gender ?? ""}
              error={errors?.gender?.[0]}
              options={[
                { value: "MALE", label: "Male" },
                { value: "FEMALE", label: "Female" },
                { value: "OTHER", label: "Other" },
              ]}
            />
            <Select
              label="Marital Status"
              name="maritalStatus"
              placeholder="Select Marital Status"
              defaultValue={initial?.maritalStatus ?? ""}
              error={errors?.maritalStatus?.[0]}
              options={[
                { value: "SINGLE", label: "Single" },
                { value: "MARRIED", label: "Married" },
                { value: "DIVORCED", label: "Divorced" },
                { value: "WIDOWED", label: "Widowed" },
              ]}
            />
            <Field
              label="National ID"
              name="nationalId"
              placeholder="e.g. 12345678"
              defaultValue={initial?.nationalId ?? undefined}
              error={errors?.nationalId?.[0]}
            />
            <Field
              label="Phone Number"
              name="phone"
              type="tel"
              placeholder="+254 700 000000"
              defaultValue={initial?.phone ?? undefined}
              error={errors?.phone?.[0]}
            />
            <Field
              label="Email"
              name="email"
              type="email"
              placeholder="john.doe@company.com"
              defaultValue={initial?.email ?? undefined}
              error={errors?.email?.[0]}
            />
            <Field
              label="Address"
              name="address"
              placeholder="e.g. Kilimani, Nairobi"
              defaultValue={initial?.address ?? undefined}
              error={errors?.address?.[0]}
            />
          </div>
        </BentoCard>

        <BentoCard
          title={
            <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Icon name="work" className="text-[20px] text-primary" />
              Employment
            </span>
          }
        >
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-gutter md:grid-cols-2">
            <Select
              label="Branch"
              name="branchId"
              placeholder="Select Branch"
              required
              value={branchId}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setBranchId(event.target.value);
                // A department from the previous branch would be rejected.
                setDepartmentId("");
              }}
              error={errors?.branchId?.[0]}
              options={options.branches.map((b) => ({
                value: String(b.id),
                label: `${b.name} (${b.code})`,
              }))}
            />
            <Select
              label="Department"
              name="departmentId"
              placeholder={
                !branchId
                  ? "Select a branch first"
                  : departments.length === 0
                    ? "No departments in this branch"
                    : "None"
              }
              disabled={!branchId}
              value={departmentId}
              onChange={(event) => setDepartmentId(event.target.value)}
              error={errors?.departmentId?.[0]}
              options={departments.map((d) => ({
                value: String(d.id),
                label: d.name,
              }))}
            />
            <Select
              label="Employment Type"
              name="employmentType"
              placeholder="Select Employment Type"
              required
              defaultValue={initial?.employmentType ?? ""}
              error={errors?.employmentType?.[0]}
              options={[
                { value: "PERMANENT", label: "Permanent" },
                { value: "CONTRACT", label: "Contract" },
                { value: "CASUAL", label: "Casual" },
                { value: "OUTSOURCED", label: "Outsourced" },
              ]}
            />
            <Field
              label="Job Title"
              name="position"
              placeholder="e.g. Software Engineer"
              defaultValue={initial?.position ?? undefined}
              error={errors?.position?.[0]}
            />
            <Field
              label="Date Hired"
              name="dateHired"
              type="date"
              defaultValue={toDateInput(initial?.dateHired)}
              error={errors?.dateHired?.[0]}
            />
            <Select
              label="Agent"
              name="agentId"
              placeholder="None — direct employee"
              defaultValue={initial?.agentId ? String(initial.agentId) : ""}
              error={errors?.agentId?.[0]}
              options={options.agents.map((a) => ({
                value: String(a.id),
                label: a.name,
              }))}
            />
          </div>
        </BentoCard>

        <BentoCard
          title={
            <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Icon name="payments" className="text-[20px] text-primary" />
              Compensation
            </span>
          }
        >
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-gutter md:grid-cols-2">
            <Field
              label="Basic Salary (KES)"
              name="basicSalary"
              type="number"
              step="0.01"
              min="0"
              placeholder="0.00"
              required
              defaultValue={initial?.basicSalary ?? undefined}
              error={errors?.basicSalary?.[0]}
            />
            <Field
              label="Bank Name"
              name="bankName"
              placeholder="e.g. Equity Bank"
              defaultValue={initial?.bankName ?? undefined}
              error={errors?.bankName?.[0]}
            />
            <Field
              label="Bank Account Number"
              name="bankAccountNumber"
              placeholder="e.g. 0123456789"
              defaultValue={initial?.bankAccountNumber ?? undefined}
              error={errors?.bankAccountNumber?.[0]}
            />
          </div>
        </BentoCard>

        <BentoCard
          title={
            <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Icon name="assured_workload" className="text-[20px] text-primary" />
              Statutory Details
            </span>
          }
        >
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-gutter md:grid-cols-2">
            <Field
              label="KRA PIN"
              name="kraPin"
              placeholder="A000000000X"
              className="uppercase"
              defaultValue={initial?.kraPin ?? undefined}
              error={errors?.kraPin?.[0]}
            />
            <Field
              label="NSSF No"
              name="nssfNumber"
              placeholder="Enter NSSF Number"
              defaultValue={initial?.nssfNumber ?? undefined}
              error={errors?.nssfNumber?.[0]}
            />
            <Field
              label="SHIF No"
              name="shifNumber"
              placeholder="Enter SHIF Number"
              defaultValue={initial?.shifNumber ?? undefined}
              error={errors?.shifNumber?.[0]}
            />
            <Field
              label="NITA No"
              name="nitaNumber"
              placeholder="Enter NITA Number"
              defaultValue={initial?.nitaNumber ?? undefined}
              error={errors?.nitaNumber?.[0]}
            />
          </div>
        </BentoCard>

        <BentoCard
          title={
            <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Icon name="emergency" className="text-[20px] text-primary" />
              Emergency Contact
            </span>
          }
        >
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-gutter md:grid-cols-2">
            <Field
              label="Contact Name"
              name="emergencyContactName"
              placeholder="e.g. Jane Doe"
              defaultValue={initial?.emergencyContactName ?? undefined}
              error={errors?.emergencyContactName?.[0]}
            />
            <Field
              label="Contact Phone"
              name="emergencyContactPhone"
              type="tel"
              placeholder="+254 700 000000"
              defaultValue={initial?.emergencyContactPhone ?? undefined}
              error={errors?.emergencyContactPhone?.[0]}
            />
          </div>
        </BentoCard>
      </div>

      {/* ──────────────────────────────── side column ──────────────────── */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-gutter lg:col-span-4">
        <BentoCard title="Guidance" className="border border-secondary-fixed bg-surface-bright">
          <ul className="flex flex-col gap-3 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant">
            <li className="flex items-start gap-2">
              <Icon name="check_circle" className="mt-0.5 text-[14px] text-tertiary-container" />
              Only four fields are required: name, branch, employment type and
              basic salary.
            </li>
            <li className="flex items-start gap-2">
              <Icon name="check_circle" className="mt-0.5 text-[14px] text-tertiary-container" />
              National ID must be unique — a duplicate is rejected by the server.
            </li>
            <li className="flex items-start gap-2">
              <Icon name="check_circle" className="mt-0.5 text-[14px] text-tertiary-container" />
              Pick the branch first; the department list follows it.
            </li>
          </ul>
        </BentoCard>

        <BentoCard title="What the server fills in" className="sticky top-24">
          <dl className="flex flex-col gap-4 font-body-md text-body-md">
            <div>
              <dt className="font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline">
                Employee Number
              </dt>
              <dd className="mt-1 text-on-surface-variant">
                Generated as <code className="text-[11px]">EMP-&lt;branch code&gt;-&lt;seq&gt;</code>{" "}
                when the record is saved.
              </dd>
            </div>
            <div>
              <dt className="font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline">
                Employment Status
              </dt>
              <dd className="mt-1 text-on-surface-variant">
                Starts as <strong>Active</strong>. Termination is a separate
                action on the employee record.
              </dd>
            </div>
          </dl>
        </BentoCard>

        <div className="flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
          <Link
            href="/hr/employees"
            className="inline-flex h-11 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-surface-container px-6 font-body-md text-body-md font-semibold text-on-surface transition-colors hover:bg-surface-container-high"
          >
            Cancel
          </Link>
          <SubmitButton
            variant="primary"
            size="lg"
            icon="save"
            loadingText="Saving…"
          >
            {mode === "create" ? "Save Employee" : "Save Changes"}
          </SubmitButton>
        </div>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
