/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ fields */

const inputClass =
  "w-full rounded-lg border border-outline-variant bg-surface-container-lowest px-3 py-2.5 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface focus:border-primary-container focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-container/20";

const labelClass =
  "mb-1 block font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline";

function Field({
  label,
  name,
  error,
  className,
  ...props
}: React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> & {
  label: string;
  name: string;
  error?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label className={labelClass} htmlFor={`employee-${name}`}>
        {label}
        {props.required ? <span className="ml-0.5 text-error">*</span> : null}
      </label>
      <input
        id={`employee-${name}`}
        name={name}
        aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
        aria-describedby={error ? `employee-${name}-error` : undefined}
        className={`${inputClass} ${className ?? ""}`}
        {...props}
      />
      {error ? (
        <p id={`employee-${name}-error`} className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-error">
          {error}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

function Select({
  label,
  name,
  options,
  placeholder,
  error,
  ...props
}: React.SelectHTMLAttributes<HTMLSelectElement> & {
  label: string;
  name: string;
  options: { value: string; label: string }[];
  placeholder: string;
  error?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label className={labelClass} htmlFor={`employee-${name}`}>
        {label}
        {props.required ? <span className="ml-0.5 text-error">*</span> : null}
      </label>
      <select
        id={`employee-${name}`}
        name={name}
        aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
        aria-describedby={error ? `employee-${name}-error` : undefined}
        className={inputClass}
        {...props}
      >
        <option value="">{placeholder}</option>
        {options.map((option) => (
          <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
            {option.label}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
      {error ? (
        <p id={`employee-${name}-error`} className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-error">
          {error}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * The department list is a function of the selected branch, not a fixed list.
 * That is a correctness requirement, not a nicety: `EmployeeServiceImpl` throws
 * when the department does not belong to the branch on the request, so a stale
 * department from a previously-selected branch would fail the whole submit.
 *
 * Every branch's departments are read once on the server and passed in as a
 * map, which costs one call per branch on page load and saves a round-trip on
 * every branch change. That is the right trade while branches number in the
 * single digits; at a hundred it would want an on-demand lookup instead.
 */

===== src/app/(portal)/hr/employees/_components/terminate-employee.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useActionState, useState } from "react";

import { terminateEmployeeAction } from "@/app/actions/employee";
import { BentoCard } from "@/components/shared/bento-card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { useActionToast } from "@/hooks/use-action-toast";
import type { ActionState } from "@/types";

/**
 * Terminate, behind a confirmation.
 *
 * This is the only way employment status changes, and it does not reverse:
 * `PUT /employees/{id}/terminate` sets the status to TERMINATED and there is no
 * endpoint that brings an employee back. The employee row is not deleted — it
 * stays, with its history, which is why the button says "terminate" rather than
 * "delete".
 *
 * The confirm step is a second click in place rather than `window.confirm`,
 * because a native dialog cannot say what is actually about to happen.
 */
export function TerminateEmployee({
  employeeId,
  employeeName,
}: {
  employeeId: number;
  employeeName: string;
}) {
  const [state, formAction] = useActionState<ActionState, FormData>(
    terminateEmployeeAction,
    { status: "idle" },
  );

  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState(false);

  useActionToast(state, { success: `${employeeName} terminated` });

  return (
    <BentoCard
      title={
        <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Icon name="warning" className="text-[20px] text-error" />
          Terminate
        </span>
      }
      className="border border-error/30"
    >
      {!confirming ? (
        <>
          <p className="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant">
            Marks this employee as terminated. Their record and history stay;
            they simply stop appearing as active staff.
          </p>
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="outline"
            className="mt-4"
            icon="block"
            onClick={() => setConfirming(true)}
          >
            Terminate employee
          </Button>
        </>
      ) : (
        <form action={formAction}>
          <input type="hidden" name="id" value={employeeId} />

          <p className="font-body-md text-body-md font-semibold text-on-surface">
            Terminate {employeeName}?
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant">
            This cannot be undone — the API has no way to reinstate an employee.
          </p>

          {state.status === "error" ? (
            <p role="alert" className="mt-3 text-[11px] text-error">
              {state.message}
            </p>
          ) : null}

          <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <Button type="submit" variant="danger" icon="check">
              Yes, terminate
            </Button>
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="ghost"
              onClick={() => setConfirming(false)}
            >
              Cancel
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      )}
    </BentoCard>
  );
}

===== src/app/actions/auth.ts =====
"use server";

import { redirect } from "next/navigation";

import { env } from "@/config/env";
import { ROLE_HOME, isRole, type Role } from "@/config/roles";
import {
  agentLoginSchema,
  changePasswordSchema,
  employeeLoginSchema,
  loginSchema,
} from "@/app/lib/definitions";
import { createSession, deleteSession, getSession } from "@/app/lib/session";
import { toActionError } from "@/app/lib/api-error";
import type { ActionState } from "@/types";

/**
 * Auth actions. Mirrors `controller/auth` + `service/auth`.
 *
 * Every action here is reachable as a direct POST regardless of which
 * component imports it, so each one validates its own input independently.
 */

/**
 * `dto/response/auth/AuthResponse`, unwrapped from its ApiResponse envelope.
 * Identical across all three login surfaces.
 */
type LoginResponse = {
  accessToken: string;
  refreshToken: string;
  userId: number;
  username: string;
  role: string;
  displayName: string;
  employeeId: number | null;
  agentId: number | null;
  branchId: number | null;
  mustChangePassword: boolean;
};

const API_ROOT = env.API_BASE_URL.replace(/\/$/, "");
const AUTH_BASE = `${API_ROOT}/auth`;

/** Deliberately uniform — never reveal whether an identifier exists. */
const INVALID_CREDENTIALS = "Incorrect credentials. Please try again.";

async function authenticate(
  endpoint: string,
  payload: Record<string, unknown>,
): Promise<LoginResponse | { error: string }> {
  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${AUTH_BASE}${endpoint}`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        Accept: "application/json",
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      cache: "no-store",
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      if (response.status === 401 || response.status === 403) {
        return { error: INVALID_CREDENTIALS };
      }
      if (response.status === 423 || response.status === 429) {
        return {
          error: "Too many attempts. Your account is locked — contact HR.",
        };
      }
      return { error: "Unable to sign in right now. Please try again." };
    }

    const envelope = (await response.json()) as {
      success?: boolean;
      message?: string;
      data?: LoginResponse | null;
    };

    if (!envelope.success || !envelope.data?.accessToken) {
      return { error: INVALID_CREDENTIALS };
    }

    return envelope.data;
  } catch {
    return { error: "Unable to reach the server. Check your connection." };
  }
}

/** Persists the session, then redirects. Never returns. */
async function establishSession(
  result: LoginResponse,
  next?: string,
): Promise<never> {
  await createSession({
    userId: result.userId,
    username: result.username,
    displayName: result.displayName ?? result.username,
    role: result.role as Role,
    employeeId: result.employeeId,
    agentId: result.agentId,
    branchId: result.branchId,
    mustChangePassword: result.mustChangePassword,
    accessToken: result.accessToken,
    refreshToken: result.refreshToken,
  });

  if (result.mustChangePassword) {
    redirect("/set-password");
  }

  // Only same-origin relative paths — blocks open redirect via ?next=
  const destination =
    next && next.startsWith("/") && !next.startsWith("//")
      ? next
      : ROLE_HOME[result.role as Role];

  redirect(destination);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ staff login */

export async function login(
  _prev: ActionState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ActionState> {
  const parsed = loginSchema.safeParse({
    username: formData.get("username"),
    password: formData.get("password"),
    next: formData.get("next") ?? undefined,
  });

  if (!parsed.success) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Check the fields below.",
      fieldErrors: parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  const { username, password, next } = parsed.data;
  const result = await authenticate("/login", { username, password });

  if ("error" in result) {
    return { status: "error", message: result.error };
  }

  if (!isRole(result.role)) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Your account has an unrecognised role. Contact an administrator.",
    };
  }

  return establishSession(result, next);
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------- employee login */

export async function employeeLogin(
  _prev: ActionState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ActionState> {
  const parsed = employeeLoginSchema.safeParse({
    employeeNumber: formData.get("employeeNumber"),
    pin: formData.get("pin"),
  });

  if (!parsed.success) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Check the fields below.",
      fieldErrors: parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  const result = await authenticate("/employee-login", parsed.data);

  if ("error" in result) {
    return { status: "error", message: result.error };
  }

  if (!isRole(result.role)) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Your account has an unrecognised role. Contact HR.",
    };
  }

  return establishSession(result);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ agent login */

export async function agentLogin(
  _prev: ActionState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ActionState> {
  const parsed = agentLoginSchema.safeParse({
    agentCode: formData.get("username"),
    password: formData.get("password"),
  });

  if (!parsed.success) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Check the fields below.",
      fieldErrors: parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  const result = await authenticate("/agent-login", parsed.data);

  if ("error" in result) {
    return { status: "error", message: result.error };
  }

  if (!isRole(result.role)) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Your account has an unrecognised role. Contact support.",
    };
  }

  return establishSession(result);
}

/* --------------------------------------------------- forced password reset */

export async function setPassword(
  _prev: ActionState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ActionState> {
  const session = await getSession();

  if (!session) {
    redirect("/login");
  }

  const parsed = changePasswordSchema.safeParse({
    currentPassword: formData.get("currentPassword"),
    newPassword: formData.get("newPassword"),
    confirmPassword: formData.get("confirmPassword"),
  });

  if (!parsed.success) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Check the fields below.",
      fieldErrors: parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  try {
    // The change-password endpoint lives on UserController, not the auth
    // controller, and is keyed by user id — `#userId == authentication
    // .principal.id or hasRole('SUPER_ADMIN')` guards it server-side.
    const response = await fetch(
      `${API_ROOT}/users/${session.userId}/change-password`,
      {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          Accept: "application/json",
          Authorization: `Bearer ${session.accessToken}`,
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          currentPassword: parsed.data.currentPassword,
          newPassword: parsed.data.newPassword,
        }),
        cache: "no-store",
      },
    );

    if (!response.ok) {
      return {
        status: "error",
        message:
          response.status === 400 || response.status === 401
            ? "Your current password is incorrect."
            : "Unable to update your password. Please try again.",
        fieldErrors:
          response.status === 400 || response.status === 401
            ? { currentPassword: ["Incorrect password."] }
            : undefined,
      };
    }
  } catch (error) {
    return toActionError(error);
  }

  // Clear must_change_password by re-issuing the cookie.
  await createSession({ ...session, mustChangePassword: false });

  redirect(ROLE_HOME[session.role]);
}

/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- logout */

export async function logout(): Promise<void> {
  await deleteSession();
  redirect("/login");
}

===== src/app/actions/document.ts =====
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";

import { toActionError } from "@/app/lib/api-error";
import { requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";
import { DOCUMENT_TYPES } from "@/types";
import type { ActionState, EmployeeDocumentResponse } from "@/types";
import {
  deleteEmployeeDocument,
  uploadEmployeeDocument,
} from "@/lib/api/document";

/**
 * Employee document writes — `EmployeeDocumentController`.
 *
 * The upload is multipart. A Server Action receives the browser's `File` on the
 * FormData directly, so there is no base64 round-trip: the File goes into a new
 * FormData and out through `api.post`, which leaves Content-Type unset so the
 * runtime can supply the multipart boundary.
 */

const MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = 10 * 1024 * 1024;

export async function uploadDocumentAction(
  _prev: ActionState<EmployeeDocumentResponse>,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ActionState<EmployeeDocumentResponse>> {
  await requirePermission("employee:document:write");

  const employeeId = Number(formData.get("employeeId"));
  const documentType = String(formData.get("documentType") ?? "");
  const file = formData.get("file");

  if (!Number.isInteger(employeeId) || employeeId <= 0) {
    return { status: "error", message: "That employee id is not valid." };
  }

  if (!DOCUMENT_TYPES.includes(documentType as (typeof DOCUMENT_TYPES)[number])) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Choose a document type.",
      fieldErrors: { documentType: ["Choose a document type."] },
    };
  }

  // A form submitted with no file chosen sends an empty File (size 0, name ""),
  // not null — so size, not presence, is what distinguishes the cases.
  if (!(file instanceof File) || file.size === 0) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Choose a file to upload.",
      fieldErrors: { file: ["Choose a file to upload."] },
    };
  }

  if (file.size > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "That file is larger than 10 MB.",
      fieldErrors: { file: ["Files must be 10 MB or smaller."] },
    };
  }

  try {
    const document = await uploadEmployeeDocument(
      employeeId,
      documentType,
      file,
    );

    revalidatePath(`/hr/employees/${employeeId}/documents`);

    return { status: "success", data: document };
  } catch (error) {
    return toActionError(error);
  }
}

/**
 * Removes a document. There is no undo — the row and the stored file are both
 * gone, so the UI confirms before calling this.
 */
export async function deleteDocumentAction(
  _prev: ActionState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ActionState> {
  await requirePermission("employee:document:write");

  const employeeId = Number(formData.get("employeeId"));
  const documentId = Number(formData.get("documentId"));

  if (!Number.isInteger(employeeId) || employeeId <= 0) {
    return { status: "error", message: "That employee id is not valid." };
  }

  if (!Number.isInteger(documentId) || documentId <= 0) {
    return { status: "error", message: "That document id is not valid." };
  }

  try {
    await deleteEmployeeDocument(employeeId, documentId);

    revalidatePath(`/hr/employees/${employeeId}/documents`);

    return { status: "success", data: undefined };
  } catch (error) {
    return toActionError(error);
  }
}

===== src/app/actions/employee.ts =====
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";

import { toActionError } from "@/app/lib/api-error";
import { requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";
import { employeeSchema } from "@/app/lib/definitions";
import {
  createEmployee,
  terminateEmployee,
  updateEmployee,
} from "@/lib/api/employee";
import type { ActionState, EmployeeResponse } from "@/types";

/**
 * HR's employee write path — `controller/employee/EmployeeController`.
 *
 * `POST /employees` and `PUT /employees/{id}` both accept the same record,
 * `EmployeeRequest`, so both actions parse with one zod schema. That schema
 * (`employeeSchema` in `app/lib/definitions.ts`) is the field-for-field mirror
 * of the DTO and is the single place the two ends are kept honest.
 *
 * Four fields are actually required by the backend: fullName, branchId,
 * employmentType, basicSalary. Everything else may be left blank, and blank
 * means "omit", not "send an empty string" — the schema's preprocessors turn
 * "" into undefined, because the DTO binds to Java enums and LocalDate and
 * would reject "".
 *
 * `employeeNumber` is deliberately absent: the service generates it as
 * `EMP-<branchCode>-<seq>`. `employmentStatus` is absent too — it is not a
 * field on the request at all.
 */

/** Pulls exactly the fields the schema knows about out of the FormData. */
function readEmployeeForm(formData: FormData) {
  return {
    fullName: formData.get("fullName"),
    nationalId: formData.get("nationalId") ?? "",
    dateOfBirth: formData.get("dateOfBirth") ?? "",
    gender: formData.get("gender") ?? "",
    maritalStatus: formData.get("maritalStatus") ?? "",
    phone: formData.get("phone") ?? "",
    email: formData.get("email") ?? "",
    address: formData.get("address") ?? "",
    kraPin: formData.get("kraPin") ?? "",
    shifNumber: formData.get("shifNumber") ?? "",
    nssfNumber: formData.get("nssfNumber") ?? "",
    nitaNumber: formData.get("nitaNumber") ?? "",
    branchId: formData.get("branchId"),
    departmentId: formData.get("departmentId") ?? "",
    agentId: formData.get("agentId") ?? "",
    position: formData.get("position") ?? "",
    employmentType: formData.get("employmentType") ?? "",
    basicSalary: formData.get("basicSalary"),
    dateHired: formData.get("dateHired") ?? "",
    bankName: formData.get("bankName") ?? "",
    bankAccountNumber: formData.get("bankAccountNumber") ?? "",
    emergencyContactName: formData.get("emergencyContactName") ?? "",
    emergencyContactPhone: formData.get("emergencyContactPhone") ?? "",
  };
}

/**
 * Creates an employee and returns the persisted record.
 *
 * Returning the created row rather than redirecting is deliberate: the
 * `employeeNumber` in the response is minted by the service, so showing it is
 * the user's proof that the row reached the database rather than just the form
 * state.
 */
export async function createEmployeeAction(
  _prev: ActionState<EmployeeResponse>,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ActionState<EmployeeResponse>> {
  // Re-checked here because a Server Action is reachable as a direct POST
  // regardless of which component imported it.
  await requirePermission("employee:write");

  const parsed = employeeSchema.safeParse(readEmployeeForm(formData));

  if (!parsed.success) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Check the fields below.",
      fieldErrors: parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  try {
    const employee = await createEmployee(parsed.data);

    // The directory is assembled per branch, so every branch's slice is stale.
    revalidatePath("/hr/employees");
    revalidatePath(`/hr/employees/${employee.id}`);

    return { status: "success", data: employee };
  } catch (error) {
    return toActionError(error);
  }
}

/** Updates an employee in place. Same payload shape as create. */
export async function updateEmployeeAction(
  _prev: ActionState<EmployeeResponse>,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ActionState<EmployeeResponse>> {
  await requirePermission("employee:write");

  const id = Number(formData.get("id"));

  if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
    return { status: "error", message: "That employee id is not valid." };
  }

  const parsed = employeeSchema.safeParse(readEmployeeForm(formData));

  if (!parsed.success) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Check the fields below.",
      fieldErrors: parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  try {
    const employee = await updateEmployee(id, parsed.data);

    revalidatePath("/hr/employees");
    revalidatePath(`/hr/employees/${id}`);

    return { status: "success", data: employee };
  } catch (error) {
    return toActionError(error);
  }
}

/**
 * Terminates an employee. SUPER_ADMIN, ADMIN and HR_MANAGER.
 *
 * This is irreversible through the API — there is no "reinstate" endpoint, and
 * the call sets `employmentStatus` to TERMINATED rather than deleting the row.
 * The UI must therefore confirm before calling it.
 */
export async function terminateEmployeeAction(
  _prev: ActionState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ActionState> {
  await requirePermission("employee:write");

  const id = Number(formData.get("id"));

  if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
    return { status: "error", message: "That employee id is not valid." };
  }

  try {
    await terminateEmployee(id);

    revalidatePath("/hr/employees");
    revalidatePath(`/hr/employees/${id}`);

    return { status: "success", data: undefined };
  } catch (error) {
    return toActionError(error);
  }
}

===== src/app/actions/leave.ts =====
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";

import { toActionError } from "@/app/lib/api-error";
import { requireAnyPermission, requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";
import { decideLeaveByHr } from "@/lib/api/leave";
import type { ActionState, LeaveRequestResponse } from "@/types";

/**
 * HR's leave decision — `PUT /leave/{id}/approve-by-hr`.
 *
 * One endpoint covers both outcomes: rejection is `approved: false` on the same
 * call, which is why this action reads a `decision` field rather than being
 * split into approve and reject functions. The backend does not require
 * `rejectionReason`, but a rejection with no reason leaves the employee with
 * nothing to read, so the form asks for one.
 */

export async function decideLeaveAction(
  _prev: ActionState<LeaveRequestResponse>,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ActionState<LeaveRequestResponse>> {
  // `leave:approve:hr` is the HR permission; SUPER_ADMIN holds the endpoint on
  // the backend without holding that permission key, so both are accepted here
  // rather than the page being unreachable for the super admin.
  await requireAnyPermission(["leave:approve:hr", "leave:read"]);

  const leaveRequestId = Number(formData.get("leaveRequestId"));
  const decision = formData.get("decision");
  const rejectionReason = String(formData.get("rejectionReason") ?? "").trim();

  if (!Number.isInteger(leaveRequestId) || leaveRequestId <= 0) {
    return { status: "error", message: "That leave request id is not valid." };
  }

  if (decision !== "approve" && decision !== "reject") {
    return { status: "error", message: "Choose whether to approve or reject." };
  }

  if (decision === "reject" && rejectionReason.length === 0) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Give a reason so the employee knows why.",
      fieldErrors: { rejectionReason: ["A reason is required to reject."] },
    };
  }

  try {
    const request = await decideLeaveByHr(leaveRequestId, {
      approved: decision === "approve",
      rejectionReason: decision === "reject" ? rejectionReason : undefined,
    });

    revalidatePath("/hr/leave");
    revalidatePath("/hr/dashboard");

    return { status: "success", data: request };
  } catch (error) {
    return toActionError(error);
  }
}

/** Kept so the module has one obvious import for the pending queue's refresh. */
export async function refreshLeaveQueue(): Promise<void> {
  await requirePermission("leave:read");
  revalidatePath("/hr/leave");
}

===== src/app/actions/profile.ts =====
"use server";

import { z } from "zod";

import { requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";
import type { ActionState } from "@/types";

/**
 * Self-service profile actions.
 *
 * ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 * NOT YET WIRED TO THE BACKEND. `PUT /employees/{id}` exists, but the
 * self-service variant ("update the employee attached to *my* session,
 * limited to the fields an employee may change about themselves") does not —
 * exposing the general endpoint here would let an employee patch fields the
 * HR screens own.
 *
 * The action still validates and still reports honestly: the user gets the
 * same field-level errors they will get once it is live, and a clear
 * "not connected yet" instead of a silent no-op. Replace the marked block
 * with the real call when the endpoint lands.
 * ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 */

const profileSchema = z.object({
  firstName: z
    .string()
    .min(1, { message: "First name is required." })
    .max(100, { message: "First name is too long." })
    .trim(),
  lastName: z
    .string()
    .min(1, { message: "Last name is required." })
    .max(100, { message: "Last name is too long." })
    .trim(),
  dateOfBirth: z
    .string()
    .regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/, { message: "Use the date picker." })
    .optional()
    .or(z.literal("")),
  gender: z.string().max(20).optional().or(z.literal("")),
  maritalStatus: z.string().max(20).optional().or(z.literal("")),
  email: z
    .string()
    .min(1, { message: "Email is required." })
    .email({ message: "Enter a valid email address." })
    .trim(),
  phone: z
    .string()
    .min(1, { message: "Phone is required." })
    .max(30, { message: "Phone number is too long." })
    .trim(),
  address: z.string().max(255, { message: "Address is too long." }).optional().or(z.literal("")),
});

export async function updateMyProfile(
  _prev: ActionState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ActionState> {
  // Re-checked here because a Server Action is reachable as a direct POST
  // regardless of which component imported it.
  await requirePermission("employee:read:self");

  const parsed = profileSchema.safeParse({
    firstName: formData.get("firstName"),
    lastName: formData.get("lastName"),
    dateOfBirth: formData.get("dateOfBirth") ?? "",
    gender: formData.get("gender") ?? "",
    maritalStatus: formData.get("maritalStatus") ?? "",
    email: formData.get("email"),
    phone: formData.get("phone"),
    address: formData.get("address") ?? "",
  });

  if (!parsed.success) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Check the fields below.",
      fieldErrors: parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  /* ---- replace with the real call once the endpoint exists --------------
   * try {
   *   await api.put("/employees/me", parsed.data);
   * } catch (error) {
   *   return toActionError(error);
   * }
   * revalidatePath("/me/profile");
   * return { status: "success", data: undefined };
   * --------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  return {
    status: "error",
    message: "Saving your profile isn't connected yet — your changes were not stored.",
  };
}

===== src/app/actions/shift.ts =====
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";

import { toActionError } from "@/app/lib/api-error";
import { requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";
import { shiftAssignmentSchema, shiftSchema } from "@/app/lib/definitions";
import {
  assignShift,
  createShift,
  updateShift,
} from "@/lib/api/shift";
import type {
  ActionState,
  ShiftAssignmentResponse,
  ShiftResponse,
} from "@/types";

/**
 * HR's shift writes — `controller/shift/ShiftController`.
 *
 * HR holds this entire controller, so there is no permission caveat here: the
 * same role that reads the roster can create, rename, retime and assign.
 */

function readShiftForm(formData: FormData) {
  return {
    name: formData.get("name"),
    type: formData.get("type") ?? "",
    startTime: formData.get("startTime") ?? "",
    endTime: formData.get("endTime") ?? "",
  };
}

/** Creates a shift. Type must be a `ShiftType` — see `SHIFT_TYPES` in @/types. */
export async function createShiftAction(
  _prev: ActionState<ShiftResponse>,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ActionState<ShiftResponse>> {
  await requirePermission("shift:write");

  const parsed = shiftSchema.safeParse(readShiftForm(formData));

  if (!parsed.success) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Check the fields below.",
      fieldErrors: parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  try {
    const shift = await createShift(parsed.data);

    revalidatePath("/hr/shifts");
    revalidatePath("/hr/shifts/roster");

    return { status: "success", data: shift };
  } catch (error) {
    return toActionError(error);
  }
}

/** Renames or retimes a shift. */
export async function updateShiftAction(
  _prev: ActionState<ShiftResponse>,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ActionState<ShiftResponse>> {
  await requirePermission("shift:write");

  const id = Number(formData.get("id"));

  if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
    return { status: "error", message: "That shift id is not valid." };
  }

  const parsed = shiftSchema.safeParse(readShiftForm(formData));

  if (!parsed.success) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Check the fields below.",
      fieldErrors: parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  try {
    const shift = await updateShift(id, parsed.data);

    revalidatePath("/hr/shifts");
    revalidatePath("/hr/shifts/roster");

    return { status: "success", data: shift };
  } catch (error) {
    return toActionError(error);
  }
}

/**
 * Assigns a shift to an employee, a department or a branch.
 *
 * At least one target is required for the assignment to mean anything — the
 * backend accepts all three null and would store an assignment to nobody, so
 * that case is refused here rather than written.
 */
export async function assignShiftAction(
  _prev: ActionState<ShiftAssignmentResponse>,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ActionState<ShiftAssignmentResponse>> {
  await requirePermission("shift:assign");

  const parsed = shiftAssignmentSchema.safeParse({
    shiftId: formData.get("shiftId"),
    employeeId: formData.get("employeeId") ?? "",
    departmentId: formData.get("departmentId") ?? "",
    branchId: formData.get("branchId") ?? "",
  });

  if (!parsed.success) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Check the fields below.",
      fieldErrors: parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  const { shiftId, employeeId, departmentId, branchId } = parsed.data;

  if (!employeeId && !departmentId && !branchId) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Choose who the shift is for — an employee, a department or a branch.",
      fieldErrors: { employeeId: ["Pick a target for this assignment."] },
    };
  }

  try {
    const assignment = await assignShift({
      shiftId,
      employeeId,
      departmentId,
      branchId,
    });

    revalidatePath("/hr/shifts/roster");

    return { status: "success", data: assignment };
  } catch (error) {
    return toActionError(error);
  }
}

===== src/app/lib/api-client.ts =====
import "server-only";

import { env } from "@/config/env";
import type { Paginated } from "@/types";

import { ApiError, toApiError } from "./api-error";
import { getSession, refreshSession } from "./session";

/**
 * Every backend controller returns `dto/response/ApiResponse` — the payload is
 * always one level down under `data`. Unwrapped here so no call site has to
 * know the envelope exists.
 */
type Envelope<T> = {
  success: boolean;
  message: string | null;
  data: T;
  timestamp?: string;
};

/**
 * Server-side fetch wrapper for the Spring Boot API.
 * Mirrors the `controller/` surface; one function per HTTP verb.
 *
 * Never import this from a Client Component — it reads the session cookie and
 * carries the backend JWT. Client data flows through Server Components or
 * Server Actions instead.
 */

type QueryValue = string | number | boolean | null | undefined;

export type ApiRequestOptions = {
  query?: Record<string, QueryValue | QueryValue[]>;
  /** Next.js cache tags, for `revalidateTag` after a mutation. */
  tags?: string[];
  /** Seconds. Omit for no-store (default on authenticated reads). */
  revalidate?: number | false;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
  /** Skip the Authorization header (login, password reset). */
  anonymous?: boolean;
};

function buildUrl(
  path: string,
  query?: ApiRequestOptions["query"],
): string {
  const base = env.API_BASE_URL.replace(/\/$/, "");
  const url = new URL(`${base}${path.startsWith("/") ? path : `/${path}`}`);

  if (query) {
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(query)) {
      if (value === undefined || value === null || value === "") continue;

      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        for (const entry of value) {
          if (entry !== undefined && entry !== null && entry !== "") {
            url.searchParams.append(key, String(entry));
          }
        }
      } else {
        url.searchParams.set(key, String(value));
      }
    }
  }

  return url.toString();
}

async function request<T>(
  method: "GET" | "POST" | "PUT" | "PATCH" | "DELETE",
  path: string,
  body?: unknown,
  options: ApiRequestOptions = {},
): Promise<T> {
  const headers = new Headers({ Accept: "application/json" });
  const isFormData = body instanceof FormData;

  if (body !== undefined && !isFormData) {
    headers.set("Content-Type", "application/json");
  }

  if (!options.anonymous) {
    const session = await getSession();

    if (!session) {
      throw new ApiError(401, "Your session has expired. Please sign in again.");
    }

    headers.set("Authorization", `Bearer ${session.accessToken}`);
  }

  const url = buildUrl(path, options.query);
  const init: RequestInit = {
    method,
    headers,
    body: isFormData ? body : body !== undefined ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
    signal: options.signal,
    // Authenticated reads are per-user; caching them across users would leak.
    cache: options.revalidate === undefined ? "no-store" : undefined,
    next:
      options.revalidate === undefined
        ? undefined
        : { revalidate: options.revalidate, tags: options.tags },
  };

  let response = await send(url, init);

  // The backend access token lasts 15 minutes against an 8-hour cookie, so a
  // 401 mid-session usually means "expired", not "signed out". Refresh once
  // and replay; a second 401 is genuine and falls through to the caller.
  if (response.status === 401 && !options.anonymous) {
    const session = await getSession();
    const accessToken = session ? await refreshSession(session) : undefined;

    if (accessToken) {
      headers.set("Authorization", `Bearer ${accessToken}`);
      response = await send(url, init);
    }
  }

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw await toApiError(response);
  }

  if (response.status === 204) {
    return undefined as T;
  }

  const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") ?? "";

  if (!contentType.includes("application/json")) {
    return (await response.text()) as T;
  }

  const payload = (await response.json()) as Envelope<T> | T;

  if (!isEnvelope<T>(payload)) {
    // Not every response is wrapped — Spring Security rejections and any
    // future raw endpoint come through bare. Pass them straight back.
    return payload;
  }

  // A handful of controllers signal refusal with `ApiResponse.error(...)` on a
  // 200 (LoanController, BiometricSyncController), so an ok status alone does
  // not mean the call succeeded.
  if (!payload.success) {
    throw new ApiError(
      response.status === 200 ? 400 : response.status,
      payload.message?.trim() || "The request was rejected.",
    );
  }

  return payload.data;
}

function isEnvelope<T>(payload: unknown): payload is Envelope<T> {
  return (
    typeof payload === "object" &&
    payload !== null &&
    "success" in payload &&
    typeof (payload as { success: unknown }).success === "boolean" &&
    "data" in payload
  );
}

/** One network hop. Translates transport failures into a 503 ApiError. */
async function send(url: string, init: RequestInit): Promise<Response> {
  try {
    return await fetch(url, init);
  } catch (cause) {
    if (cause instanceof Error && cause.name === "AbortError") throw cause;
    throw new ApiError(503, "Unable to reach the server.");
  }
}

export const api = {
  get: <T>(path: string, options?: ApiRequestOptions) =>
    request<T>("GET", path, undefined, options),

  /** Convenience wrapper for endpoints returning Spring `Page<T>`. */
  getPage: <T>(path: string, options?: ApiRequestOptions) =>
    request<Paginated<T>>("GET", path, undefined, options),

  post: <T>(path: string, body?: unknown, options?: ApiRequestOptions) =>
    request<T>("POST", path, body, options),

  put: <T>(path: string, body?: unknown, options?: ApiRequestOptions) =>
    request<T>("PUT", path, body, options),

  patch: <T>(path: string, body?: unknown, options?: ApiRequestOptions) =>
    request<T>("PATCH", path, body, options),

  delete: <T>(path: string, options?: ApiRequestOptions) =>
    request<T>("DELETE", path, undefined, options),
};

/** Streams a PDF from `service/payroll/documents` for download. */
export async function fetchDocument(
  path: string,
  options: ApiRequestOptions = {},
): Promise<{ blob: Blob; filename: string }> {
  const session = await getSession();

  if (!session) {
    throw new ApiError(401, "Your session has expired. Please sign in again.");
  }

  const response = await fetch(buildUrl(path, options.query), {
    headers: {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${session.accessToken}`,
      Accept: "application/pdf",
    },
    cache: "no-store",
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw await toApiError(response);
  }

  const disposition = response.headers.get("content-disposition") ?? "";
  const match = /filename="?([^"]+)"?/.exec(disposition);

  return {
    blob: await response.blob(),
    filename: match?.[1] ?? "document.pdf",
  };
}

===== src/app/lib/api-error.ts =====
import "server-only";

/**
 * Error envelope parsing. Mirrors `exception/GlobalExceptionHandler`.
 *
 * Parsed in one place so no call site has to guess the response shape.
 */

/** Shape emitted by the Spring GlobalExceptionHandler (`ApiResponse.error`). */
export type ApiErrorBody = {
  success?: boolean;
  message?: string;
  timestamp?: string;
  /**
   * `@Valid` failures land here — the handler puts the field→message map in
   * the envelope's `data` slot rather than a dedicated `errors` key.
   */
  data?: Record<string, string | string[]> | null;
  /** Spring Boot's own error page shape, for anything short of a controller. */
  status?: number;
  error?: string;
  path?: string;
  errors?: Record<string, string | string[]>;
};

export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;
  readonly fieldErrors?: Record<string, string[]>;
  readonly path?: string;

  constructor(
    status: number,
    message: string,
    fieldErrors?: Record<string, string[]>,
    path?: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
    this.status = status;
    this.fieldErrors = fieldErrors;
    this.path = path;
  }

  get isUnauthorized(): boolean {
    return this.status === 401;
  }

  get isForbidden(): boolean {
    return this.status === 403;
  }

  get isNotFound(): boolean {
    return this.status === 404;
  }

  get isValidation(): boolean {
    return this.status === 400 || this.status === 422;
  }

  /** 409 — optimistic-lock or duplicate-key collision. */
  get isConflict(): boolean {
    return this.status === 409;
  }

  get isServer(): boolean {
    return this.status >= 500;
  }
}

function normalizeFieldErrors(
  errors: Record<string, string | string[]> | null | undefined,
): Record<string, string[]> | undefined {
  if (!errors) return undefined;

  const entries = Object.entries(errors).filter(
    ([, value]) =>
      typeof value === "string" ||
      (Array.isArray(value) && value.every((v) => typeof v === "string")),
  );

  if (entries.length === 0) return undefined;

  return Object.fromEntries(
    entries.map(([field, value]) => [
      field,
      Array.isArray(value) ? value : [value],
    ]),
  );
}

const FALLBACK_MESSAGES: Record<number, string> = {
  400: "The request was invalid.",
  401: "Your session has expired. Please sign in again.",
  403: "You do not have permission to perform this action.",
  404: "The requested record was not found.",
  409: "This record was changed by someone else. Reload and try again.",
  422: "Some fields need attention.",
  500: "Something went wrong on the server.",
  503: "The service is temporarily unavailable.",
};

/** Builds an ApiError from a failed Response. Never throws. */
export async function toApiError(response: Response): Promise<ApiError> {
  let body: ApiErrorBody | undefined;

  try {
    body = (await response.json()) as ApiErrorBody;
  } catch {
    // Empty or non-JSON body (gateway timeout, HTML error page).
  }

  const message =
    body?.message?.trim() ||
    body?.error?.trim() ||
    FALLBACK_MESSAGES[response.status] ||
    `Request failed with status ${response.status}.`;

  return new ApiError(
    response.status,
    message,
    normalizeFieldErrors(asFieldMap(body?.data) ?? body?.errors),
    body?.path,
  );
}

/**
 * `data` is the envelope's generic payload slot, so on a validation failure it
 * holds the field→message map but on any other error it is null — or, for a
 * hand-rolled error response, could be anything at all. Only a plain object
 * survives; a string would otherwise index into per-character "fields".
 */
function asFieldMap(
  data: unknown,
): Record<string, string | string[]> | undefined {
  if (typeof data !== "object" || data === null || Array.isArray(data)) {
    return undefined;
  }

  return data as Record<string, string | string[]>;
}

/**
 * Converts any thrown value into an `ActionState` error for `useActionState`.
 * Keeps raw internals out of the UI.
 */
export function toActionError(error: unknown): {
  status: "error";
  message: string;
  fieldErrors?: Record<string, string[]>;
} {
  if (error instanceof ApiError) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: error.message,
      fieldErrors: error.fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  return {
    status: "error",
    message: "Unable to reach the server. Check your connection and try again.",
  };
}

===== src/app/lib/dal.ts =====
import "server-only";

import { cache } from "react";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";

import {
  ROLE_HOME,
  canAccessPath,
  hasAnyPermission,
  hasPermission,
  type Permission,
  type Role,
} from "@/config/roles";
import type { CurrentUser } from "@/types";

import { getSession, type SessionPayload } from "./session";

/**
 * Data Access Layer. Mirrors `security/AccessGuard`.
 *
 * Every protected page and Server Action calls through here. Wrapped in
 * React `cache()` so repeated calls inside one render pass hit once.
 *
 * This is a UX gate, not the security boundary — the Spring AccessGuard
 * re-authorizes every request regardless of what happens here.
 */

/** Redirects to /login when there is no valid session. */
export const verifySession = cache(async (): Promise<SessionPayload> => {
  const session = await getSession();

  if (!session) {
    redirect("/login");
  }

  // Forced rotation on `users.must_change_password`.
  if (session.mustChangePassword) {
    redirect("/set-password");
  }

  return session;
});

/** Returns the session without redirecting. For optional-auth surfaces. */
export const getOptionalSession = cache(
  async (): Promise<SessionPayload | undefined> => getSession(),
);

/**
 * Session check for the /set-password route itself.
 *
 * `verifySession()` bounces a flagged session to /set-password, so that page
 * cannot call it without looping. This variant requires a valid session but
 * tolerates the `mustChangePassword` flag.
 */
export const verifySessionAllowingPasswordChange = cache(
  async (): Promise<SessionPayload> => {
    const session = await getSession();

    if (!session) {
      redirect("/login");
    }

    return session;
  },
);

/** Client-safe projection. Never leaks `accessToken`. */
export const getCurrentUser = cache(async (): Promise<CurrentUser> => {
  const session = await verifySession();

  return {
    id: session.userId,
    username: session.username,
    displayName: session.username,
    role: session.role,
    employeeId: session.employeeId,
    agentId: session.agentId,
    branchId: session.branchId,
    active: true,
    mustChangePassword: session.mustChangePassword,
  };
});

/** Backend JWT for outbound API calls. Server-side only. */
export const getAccessToken = cache(async (): Promise<string> => {
  const session = await verifySession();
  return session.accessToken;
});

/**
 * Asserts the caller holds `permission`, else bounces to their own home.
 * Use at the top of every Server Action and protected page.
 */
export async function requirePermission(
  permission: Permission,
): Promise<SessionPayload> {
  const session = await verifySession();

  if (!hasPermission(session.role, permission)) {
    redirect(ROLE_HOME[session.role]);
  }

  return session;
}

export async function requireAnyPermission(
  permissions: readonly Permission[],
): Promise<SessionPayload> {
  const session = await verifySession();

  if (!hasAnyPermission(session.role, permissions)) {
    redirect(ROLE_HOME[session.role]);
  }

  return session;
}

/** Asserts the caller's role matches one of `roles`. */
export async function requireRole(
  roles: readonly Role[],
): Promise<SessionPayload> {
  const session = await verifySession();

  if (!roles.includes(session.role)) {
    redirect(ROLE_HOME[session.role]);
  }

  return session;
}

/** Asserts `pathname` is inside the namespace the caller's role owns. */
export async function requireNamespace(
  pathname: string,
): Promise<SessionPayload> {
  const session = await verifySession();

  if (!canAccessPath(session.role, pathname)) {
    redirect(ROLE_HOME[session.role]);
  }

  return session;
}

/**
 * Branch scoping for BRANCH_MANAGER. Roles with company-wide read get `null`
 * (no filter); a branch-scoped role gets its own `branchId`.
 */
export async function getBranchScope(): Promise<number | null> {
  const session = await verifySession();

  if (hasPermission(session.role, "branch:read")) {
    return null;
  }

  return session.branchId;
}

===== src/app/lib/definitions.ts =====
import { z } from "zod";

/**
 * Validation schemas mirroring the backend `dto/request` records.
 *
 * NOTE: this project has zod 3.25.x. Use `z.string().email({ message })`,
 * not the zod 4 `z.email({ error })` form — the latter is undefined here.
 *
 * These run client-side for fast feedback AND inside Server Actions before
 * hitting the API. Bean Validation on the Spring side stays authoritative.
 */

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ auth -- */

export const loginSchema = z.object({
  // `users.username` is the credential, not email.
  username: z
    .string()
    .min(1, { message: "Username is required." })
    .max(100, { message: "Username is too long." })
    .trim(),
  password: z.string().min(1, { message: "Password is required." }),
  next: z.string().optional(),
});

export type LoginInput = z.infer<typeof loginSchema>;

/**
 * Employee self-service credential: `employees.employee_number` + 4-digit PIN.
 *
 * A 4-digit PIN is only 10,000 combinations. Attempt throttling and lockout
 * must be enforced server-side — nothing here can rate-limit an attacker.
 */
export const employeeLoginSchema = z.object({
  employeeNumber: z
    .string()
    .min(1, { message: "Employee number is required." })
    .max(30, { message: "Employee number is too long." })
    .trim(),
  pin: z
    .string()
    .length(4, { message: "Enter all 4 digits." })
    .regex(/^\d{4}$/, { message: "PIN must be 4 digits." }),
});

export type EmployeeLoginInput = z.infer<typeof employeeLoginSchema>;

/**
 * Agent portal credential.
 *
 * NOTE: `agents` has no code column in V1 — only id, name and kra_pin. The
 * backend needs a unique agent code before this can authenticate.
 */
export const agentLoginSchema = z.object({
  agentCode: z
    .string()
    .min(1, { message: "Agent code is required." })
    .max(50, { message: "Agent code is too long." })
    .trim(),
  password: z.string().min(1, { message: "Password is required." }),
});

export type AgentLoginInput = z.infer<typeof agentLoginSchema>;

const passwordRules = z
  .string()
  .min(8, { message: "Use at least 8 characters." })
  .max(72, { message: "Use at most 72 characters." })
  .regex(/[A-Z]/, { message: "Include an uppercase letter." })
  .regex(/[a-z]/, { message: "Include a lowercase letter." })
  .regex(/[0-9]/, { message: "Include a number." });

export const changePasswordSchema = z
  .object({
    currentPassword: z.string().min(1, { message: "Current password is required." }),
    newPassword: passwordRules,
    confirmPassword: z.string().min(1, { message: "Confirm your new password." }),
  })
  .refine((data) => data.newPassword === data.confirmPassword, {
    message: "Passwords do not match.",
    path: ["confirmPassword"],
  })
  .refine((data) => data.newPassword !== data.currentPassword, {
    message: "New password must differ from the current one.",
    path: ["newPassword"],
  });

export const forgotPasswordSchema = z.object({
  username: z.string().min(1, { message: "Username is required." }).trim(),
});

export const resetPasswordSchema = z
  .object({
    token: z.string().min(1),
    newPassword: passwordRules,
    confirmPassword: z.string().min(1, { message: "Confirm your new password." }),
  })
  .refine((data) => data.newPassword === data.confirmPassword, {
    message: "Passwords do not match.",
    path: ["confirmPassword"],
  });

/* -------------------------------------------------------------- employee -- */

const optionalString = z
  .string()
  .trim()
  .max(255)
  .optional()
  .or(z.literal(""))
  .transform((v) => (v === "" ? undefined : v));

// An HTML <select> submits "" when nothing is chosen, and an optional numeric
// id must arrive as absent rather than as 0 — `z.coerce.number()` turns "" into
// 0, which then trips `.positive()` and reports a validation error for a field
// the user deliberately left blank.
const optionalId = z.preprocess(
  (v) => (v === "" || v === null || v === undefined ? undefined : v),
  z.coerce.number().int().positive().optional()
);

// Same reasoning for optional enum selects, and for optional dates: the
// backend binds these to Java enums and LocalDate, so an empty string is a 400
// rather than a blank field.
const optionalEnum = (values: [string, ...string[]]) =>
  z.preprocess(
    (v) => (v === "" || v === null ? undefined : v),
    z.enum(values).optional()
  );

const optionalDate = z
  .string()
  .optional()
  .or(z.literal(""))
  .transform((v) => (v === "" ? undefined : v));

/* -------------------------------------------------------------- employee -- */

// Field-for-field against EmployeeRequest in the backend
// (dto/request/employee/EmployeeRequest.java). Only four fields are @NotNull
// there — fullName, branchId, employmentType, basicSalary — so everything else
// is genuinely optional.
//
// Two fields the backend owns and this schema must NOT contain:
//   employeeNumber  generated server-side as EMP-<branchCode>-<seq>, with a
//                   collision retry. Sending one is at best ignored.
//   employmentStatus not in the request DTO at all; the service defaults it.
export const employeeSchema = z.object({
  fullName: z
    .string()
    .min(2, { message: "Full name is required." })
    .max(150, { message: "Full name is too long." })
    .trim(),
  nationalId: z.string().max(30).trim().optional(),
  dateOfBirth: optionalDate,
  gender: optionalEnum(["MALE", "FEMALE", "OTHER"]),
  maritalStatus: optionalEnum([
    "SINGLE",
    "MARRIED",
    "DIVORCED",
    "WIDOWED",
  ]),
  phone: optionalString,
  email: z
    .string()
    .email({ message: "Enter a valid email address." })
    .optional()
    .or(z.literal(""))
    .transform((v) => (v === "" ? undefined : v)),
  address: optionalString,
  kraPin: z.string().max(20).trim().optional(),
  shifNumber: z.string().max(20).trim().optional(),
  nssfNumber: z.string().max(20).trim().optional(),
  nitaNumber: z.string().max(20).trim().optional(),
  branchId: z.coerce.number().int().positive({ message: "Select a branch." }),
  departmentId: optionalId,
  agentId: optionalId,
  position: optionalString,
  // EmploymentType enum: PERMANENT, CONTRACT, CASUAL, OUTSOURCED.
  // There is no INTERN — offering it was a guaranteed 400.
  employmentType: z.enum(
    ["PERMANENT", "CONTRACT", "CASUAL", "OUTSOURCED"],
    { message: "Select an employment type." }
  ),
  basicSalary: z.coerce
    .number()
    .nonnegative({ message: "Salary cannot be negative." }),
  dateHired: optionalDate,
  bankName: optionalString,
  bankAccountNumber: optionalString,
  emergencyContactName: optionalString,
  emergencyContactPhone: optionalString,
});

export type EmployeeInput = z.infer<typeof employeeSchema>;

/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- leave -- */

export const leaveRequestSchema = z
  .object({
    employeeId: z.coerce.number().int().positive().optional(),
    leaveTypeId: z.coerce
      .number()
      .int()
      .positive({ message: "Select a leave type." }),
    startDate: z.string().min(1, { message: "Start date is required." }),
    endDate: z.string().min(1, { message: "End date is required." }),
    reason: z.string().max(255).trim().optional(),
  })
  .refine((data) => data.endDate >= data.startDate, {
    message: "End date cannot be before the start date.",
    path: ["endDate"],
  });

export const leaveDecisionSchema = z

    decision: z.enum(["APPROVE", "REJECT"]),
    rejectionReason: z.string().max(255).trim().optional(),

    (data) => data.decision !== "REJECT" || !!data.rejectionReason?.length,

export const loanRequestSchema = z.object({

  email: z

===== src/app/lib/session.ts =====
import "server-only";

import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { SignJWT, jwtVerify } from "jose";

import { env } from "@/config/env";
import { isRole, type Role } from "@/config/roles";

/**
 * Session management. Mirrors `security/JwtService`.
 *
 * The Spring API issues its own JWT. We wrap it in a second, httpOnly cookie
 * signed with SESSION_SECRET so the backend token never reaches the browser —
 * no localStorage, nothing readable from JS. `apiFetch` unwraps it server-side
 * and attaches it as `Authorization: Bearer`.
 */

const SESSION_COOKIE = "dhwarsh_session";

/**
 * Lazily derives and caches the signing key on first use, rather than at
 * module scope. Reading `env.SESSION_SECRET` eagerly at import time would
 * throw the moment anything imports this module — including `next build`
 * collecting page data — before a request context ever exists.
 */
let cachedKey: Uint8Array | undefined;
function getEncodedKey(): Uint8Array {
  if (!cachedKey) {
    cachedKey = new TextEncoder().encode(env.SESSION_SECRET);
  }
  return cachedKey;
}

export type SessionPayload = {
  userId: number;
  username: string;
  displayName: string;
  role: Role;
  employeeId: number | null;
  agentId: number | null;
  branchId: number | null;
  mustChangePassword: boolean;
  /** Backend JWT. Server-side only — never send to a Client Component. */
  accessToken: string;
  /**
   * Longer-lived backend token, exchanged for a fresh access token when the
   * access token expires mid-session. Same server-only rule as above.
   */
  refreshToken: string;
  expiresAt: string;
};

export async function encrypt(payload: SessionPayload): Promise<string> {
  return new SignJWT({ ...payload })
    .setProtectedHeader({ alg: "HS256" })
    .setIssuedAt()
    .setExpirationTime("8h")
    .sign(getEncodedKey());
}

export async function decrypt(
  session: string | undefined = "",
): Promise<SessionPayload | undefined> {
  try {
    const { payload } = await jwtVerify(session, getEncodedKey(), {
      algorithms: ["HS256"],
    });

    // Trust nothing that comes back out of the cookie without re-checking.
    if (
      typeof payload.userId !== "number" ||
      typeof payload.username !== "string" ||
      typeof payload.accessToken !== "string" ||
      typeof payload.refreshToken !== "string" ||
      !isRole(payload.role)
    ) {
      return undefined;
    }

    return payload as unknown as SessionPayload;
  } catch {
    // Expired or tampered — treat as signed out.
    return undefined;
  }
}

export async function createSession(
  payload: Omit<SessionPayload, "expiresAt">,
): Promise<void> {
  const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + 8 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  const session = await encrypt({ ...payload, expiresAt: expiresAt.toISOString() });
  const cookieStore = await cookies();

  cookieStore.set(SESSION_COOKIE, session, {
    httpOnly: true,
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
    expires: expiresAt,
    sameSite: "lax",
    path: "/",
  });
}

export async function getSession(): Promise<SessionPayload | undefined> {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  return decrypt(cookieStore.get(SESSION_COOKIE)?.value);
}

/**
 * Trades the refresh token for a fresh pair via `POST /auth/refresh`.
 *
 * The backend access token lives 15 minutes while this cookie lives 8 hours,
 * so without this every session would start failing a quarter-hour in.
 * Returns the new access token, or undefined if the refresh token is itself
 * spent — the caller should then treat the session as ended.
 *
 * Deliberately uses raw `fetch` rather than `api` to avoid an import cycle:
 * api-client depends on this module for the token it attaches.
 */
export async function refreshSession(
  session: SessionPayload,
): Promise<string | undefined> {
  let payload: { data?: RefreshedTokens } | undefined;

  try {
    const response = await fetch(
      `${env.API_BASE_URL.replace(/\/$/, "")}/auth/refresh`,
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          Accept: "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ refreshToken: session.refreshToken }),
        cache: "no-store",
      },
    );

    if (!response.ok) return undefined;

    payload = (await response.json()) as { data?: RefreshedTokens };
  } catch {
    return undefined;
  }

  const tokens = payload?.data;

  if (
    typeof tokens?.accessToken !== "string" ||
    typeof tokens?.refreshToken !== "string"
  ) {
    return undefined;
  }

  try {
    await createSession({ ...session, ...tokens });
  } catch {
    // Server Components render with a read-only cookie store, so persisting
    // fails there. The caller still gets the fresh token for this request;
    // the next Server Action or Route Handler re-persists it.
  }

  return tokens.accessToken;
}

type RefreshedTokens = { accessToken: string; refreshToken: string };

export async function deleteSession(): Promise<void> {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  cookieStore.delete(SESSION_COOKIE);
}

export { SESSION_COOKIE };
===== src/components/features/branch/approval-queue.tsx =====
// src/components/features/branch/approval-queue.tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";

import { EmptyState } from "@/components/shared/empty-state";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { initials as toInitials } from "@/lib/format";
import { toast } from "@/lib/toast";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export type ApprovalType = "Leave" | "Overtime" | "Advance" | "Loan" | "Expense";

export interface ApprovalRequest {
  id: string;
  employeeName: string;
  avatarUrl?: string;
  /** Overrides the name-derived fallback when there is no photo. */
  initials?: string;
  type: ApprovalType;
  /** Material Symbols ligature summarising the request, e.g. "event". */
  detailIcon: string;
  detailText: string;
  /** Relative submission label, e.g. "Submitted 2 hours ago". */
  submittedAt: string;
  highPriority?: boolean;
}

const TYPE_STYLES: Record<ApprovalType, string> = {
  Leave: "bg-amber-main/10 text-amber-main",
  Overtime: "bg-error/10 text-error",
  Advance: "bg-blue/10 text-blue",
  Loan: "bg-blue/10 text-blue",
  Expense: "bg-green-main/10 text-green-main",
};

export function ApprovalQueue({ requests }: { requests: ApprovalRequest[] }) {
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(requests);

  /**
   * Optimistic removal with an undo affordance.
   *
   * The decision is not persisted yet (no approvals endpoint), so the toast
   * says so rather than claiming the request was approved. Undo restores the
   * row in place — an approval dismissed by a mis-tap should not need a page
   * reload to get back.
   */
  function resolve(request: ApprovalRequest, decision: "approved" | "rejected") {
    const index = pending.findIndex((item) => item.id === request.id);
    setPending((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== request.id));

    toast.warning(`${request.type} ${decision} — not saved`, {
      description: `${request.employeeName}'s request left your queue, but approvals aren't connected to the backend yet.`,
      action: {
        label: "Undo",
        // Splice back at the original index so the queue keeps its order.
        onClick: () =>
          setPending((prev) => {
            if (prev.some((item) => item.id === request.id)) return prev;
            const next = [...prev];
            next.splice(Math.min(index, next.length), 0, request);
            return next;
          }),
      },
    });
  }

  if (pending.length === 0) {
    return (
      <EmptyState
        title="Queue is clear"
        description="Every request from your branch team has been actioned."
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <ul className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      {pending.map((request) => (
        <li
          key={request.id}
          className={cn(
            "flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 rounded-2xl bg-white p-4 shadow-ambient-1",
            request.highPriority && "border-l-4 border-error",
          )}
        >
          {request.avatarUrl ? (
            // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element -- avatars are remote and unoptimised upstream
            <img
              src={request.avatarUrl}
              alt=""
              className="size-10 shrink-0 rounded-full object-cover"
            />
          ) : (
            <span
              aria-hidden
              className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-surface-container-highest text-xs font-bold text-on-surface-variant"
            >
              {request.initials ?? toInitials(request.employeeName)}
            </span>
          )}

          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <p className="truncate font-semibold text-on-surface">{request.employeeName}</p>
              <span
                className={cn(
                  "shrink-0 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium",
                  TYPE_STYLES[request.type],
                )}
              >
                {request.type}
              </span>
              {request.highPriority ? (
                <span className="shrink-0 rounded bg-error/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium text-error">
                  High priority
                </span>
              ) : null}
            </div>

            <p className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-on-surface-variant">
              <Icon name={request.detailIcon} className="text-[16px]" />
              <span className="min-w-0 truncate">{request.detailText}</span>
            </p>

            {request.submittedAt ? (
              <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-outline">{request.submittedAt}</p>
            ) : null}
          </div>

          <div className="flex w-full shrink-0 gap-2 sm:w-auto">
            <button
              type="button"
              data-live=""
              onClick={() => resolve(request, "rejected")}
              className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-md border border-outline-variant/50 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:bg-surface-container-low sm:flex-initial sm:py-1.5"
            >
              <Icon name="close" className="text-[18px]" />
              Reject
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              data-live=""
              onClick={() => resolve(request, "approved")}
              className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-primary px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white transition-colors hover:bg-primary/90 sm:flex-initial sm:py-1.5"
            >
              <Icon name="check" className="text-[18px]" />
              Approve
            </button>
          </div>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}

===== src/components/layout/app-rail.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect } from "react";

import { logout } from "@/app/actions/auth";
import { isNavItemActive, navigationFor } from "@/config/navigation";
import { ROLE_LABEL, type Role } from "@/config/roles";
import { siteConfig } from "@/config/site";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { useNav } from "@/components/layout/nav-context";
import { BrandMark } from "@/components/shared/brand-logo";
import { toast } from "@/lib/toast";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

/**
 * The navigation rail.
 *
 * Two widths on desktop: a 96px icon rail and a 264px labelled sidebar. The
 * expanded state pushes the content column (the rail is in normal flow from
 * `md` up, so widening it reflows the page rather than covering it) — the
 * layout's own `min-w-0` is what lets the content actually shrink instead of
 * overflowing.
 *
 * Below `md` the rail is off-canvas and always renders in its labelled form:
 * a drawer has room for text, and an icon-only drawer would be strictly worse
 * than the thing it replaces.
 *
 * Role-scoped: renders only the routes belonging to `role`, so the rail can
 * never surface a link outside that role's namespace. Menu visibility only —
 * the DAL re-checks on every page.
 *
 * The item list scrolls independently of the header and footer, because
 * SUPER_ADMIN owns eight routes and the rail must not push its own footer
 * off-screen on a short viewport.
 */
export function AppRail({ role }: { role: Role }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const { open, close, expanded, toggleExpanded } = useNav();
  const items = navigationFor(role);

  // Close the drawer when the route changes. Without this it stays open over
  // the page the user just navigated to.
  useEffect(() => {
    close();
  }, [pathname, close]);

  // Labels show in the drawer regardless of the desktop width preference.
  const showLabels = expanded;

  return (
    <>
      {/* Scrim. Mobile only — the rail is in flow from md up. */}
      <div
        aria-hidden
        onClick={close}
        className={cn(
          "fixed inset-0 z-40 bg-navy/40 backdrop-blur-[2px] transition-opacity duration-300 md:hidden",
          open ? "opacity-100" : "pointer-events-none opacity-0",
        )}
      />

      <nav
        aria-label="Main navigation"
        className={cn(
          "sidebar-gradient fixed left-0 top-0 z-50 flex h-dvh w-[264px] shrink-0 flex-col py-6 shadow-lg",
          "transition-[transform,width] duration-300 ease-out motion-reduce:transition-none",
          "md:relative md:h-full md:translate-x-0",
          showLabels ? "md:w-[264px]" : "md:w-rail-width",
          open ? "translate-x-0" : "-translate-x-full",
        )}
      >
        {/* Brand + desktop width toggle */}
        <div
          className={cn(
            "mb-6 flex shrink-0 items-center gap-3 px-4",
            showLabels ? "justify-between" : "md:flex-col md:justify-center md:gap-2 md:px-2",
          )}
        >
          <Link
            href="/"
            className={cn(
              "flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3",
              showLabels ? "" : "md:flex-col md:gap-1 md:text-center",
            )}
          >
            {/* Emblem only: the rail collapses to 96px, where the lockup's
                wordmark would be unreadable. The adjacent text names the
                brand, so the mark itself is decorative. */}
            <BrandMark
              eager
              sizes="44px"
              className="size-11 rounded-xl shadow-sm ring-1 ring-white/25"
            />
            <span
              className={cn(
                "flex min-w-0 flex-col",
                showLabels ? "" : "md:items-center",
              )}
            >
              <span
                className={cn(
                  "truncate text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-on-primary",
                  showLabels ? "" : "md:font-label-rail md:text-label-rail",
                )}
              >
                {siteConfig.shortName}
              </span>
              <span className="truncate text-[10px] leading-tight text-on-primary/70">
                {ROLE_LABEL[role]}
              </span>
            </span>
          </Link>

          {/* Desktop only. On mobile the drawer is dismissed by the scrim,
              Escape, or picking a route — a second control would just crowd
              the header. */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={toggleExpanded}
            aria-label={showLabels ? "Collapse sidebar" : "Expand sidebar"}
            aria-expanded={showLabels}
            title={showLabels ? "Collapse sidebar" : "Expand sidebar"}
            className="hidden size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-on-primary/70 transition-colors hover:bg-surface-container-lowest/15 hover:text-on-primary focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-on-primary/60 md:flex"
          >
            <Icon
              name={showLabels ? "menu_open" : "menu"}
              className="text-[20px]"
            />
          </button>

          {/* Mobile drawer dismiss. */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={close}
            aria-label="Close navigation"
            className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-on-primary/70 transition-colors hover:bg-surface-container-lowest/15 hover:text-on-primary md:hidden"
          >
            <Icon name="close" className="text-[20px]" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Routes */}
        <ul className="no-scrollbar flex flex-1 flex-col gap-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 md:px-2">
          {items.map((item) => {
            const active = isNavItemActive(pathname, item.href);

            return (
              <li key={item.href}>
                <Link
                  href={item.href}
                  onClick={close}
                  aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
                  /* Native tooltip so a collapsed rail is still identifiable
                     without hover-only custom chrome. */
                  title={showLabels ? undefined : item.title}
                  className={cn(
                    "group relative flex items-center rounded-xl transition-colors focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-on-primary/60",
                    // Drawer and expanded rail: icon beside label.
                    "gap-3 px-3 py-2.5",
                    // Collapsed desktop rail: icon above label, centred.
                    showLabels
                      ? ""
                      : "md:flex-col md:justify-center md:gap-1 md:px-2 md:py-2 md:text-center",
                    active
                      ? "bg-surface-container-lowest/20 text-on-primary"
                      : "text-on-primary/70 hover:bg-surface-container-lowest/10 hover:text-on-primary",
                  )}
                >
                  {/* Active marker on the rail edge */}
                  {active ? (
                    <span
                      aria-hidden
                      className="absolute left-0 top-1/2 h-8 w-1 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-r-full bg-on-primary"
                    />
                  ) : null}

                  <Icon
                    name={item.icon}
                    filled={active}
                    className="shrink-0 text-[22px]"
                  />
                  <span
                    className={cn(
                      "min-w-0 truncate text-sm font-medium leading-tight",
                      // Only the collapsed desktop rail drops to the 10px
                      // two-line label; the drawer has room for real text.
                      showLabels
                        ? ""
                        : "md:whitespace-normal md:font-label-rail md:text-label-rail",
                    )}
                  >
                    {item.title}
                  </span>
                </Link>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="mt-auto flex shrink-0 flex-col gap-1 px-3 pt-4 md:px-2">
          {/* Not a Link: there is no /help route yet, and a rail item that
              404s is worse than one that says "not built". */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              close();
              toast.notImplemented("Help centre");
            }}
            title={showLabels ? undefined : "Help"}
            className={cn(
              "flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-on-primary/70 transition-colors hover:bg-surface-container-lowest/10 hover:text-on-primary focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-on-primary/60",
              showLabels
                ? ""
                : "md:flex-col md:justify-center md:gap-1 md:px-2 md:py-2 md:text-center",
            )}
          >
            <Icon name="help" className="shrink-0 text-[22px]" />
            <span
              className={cn(
                "text-sm font-medium",
                showLabels ? "" : "md:font-label-rail md:text-label-rail",
              )}
            >
              Help
            </span>
          </button>

          {/* Server Action — a real POST, so signing out survives JS being off
              and cannot be triggered by a cross-origin GET. */}
          <form action={logout}>
            <button
              type="submit"
              title={showLabels ? undefined : "Logout"}
              className={cn(
                "flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-on-primary/70 transition-colors hover:bg-surface-container-lowest/10 hover:text-on-primary focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-on-primary/60",
                showLabels
                  ? ""
                  : "md:flex-col md:justify-center md:gap-1 md:px-2 md:py-2 md:text-center",
              )}
            >
              <Icon name="logout" className="shrink-0 text-[22px]" />
              <span
                className={cn(
                  "text-sm font-medium",
                  showLabels ? "" : "md:font-label-rail md:text-label-rail",
                )}
              >
                Logout
              </span>
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>
      </nav>
    </>
  );
}

===== src/components/layout/nav-context.tsx =====
"use client";

import {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useContext,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useState,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";

/**
 * Navigation shell state — two independent axes, deliberately not merged.
 *
 * `open` is the mobile drawer. The rail is off-canvas below `md`, so this is
 * a transient overlay: it always starts closed, closes on route change, and is
 * never persisted. The hamburger that drives it lives in the top bar rather
 * than inside the rail, which is why the state is lifted here.
 *
 * `expanded` is the desktop width. The rail is always visible from `md` up and
 * toggles between a 96px icon rail and a 264px labelled sidebar that pushes the
 * content column rather than covering it. This one IS persisted, because a
 * width preference that resets on every navigation is worse than no toggle.
 *
 * Persistence is a plain `document.cookie` write, not a Server Action. The
 * layout is a Server Component that reads the cookie to pick the first paint,
 * so a client-only write keeps the toggle instant while still surviving reload
 * with no flash of the wrong width. It is a UI preference, so it is
 * deliberately not httpOnly — nothing here is a security boundary.
 */

export const SIDEBAR_COOKIE = "sidebar_expanded";
const COOKIE_MAX_AGE = 60 * 60 * 24 * 365;

type NavState = {
  /** Mobile drawer. */
  open: boolean;
  toggle: () => void;
  close: () => void;
  /** Desktop sidebar width. */
  expanded: boolean;
  toggleExpanded: () => void;
};

const NavContext = createContext<NavState | null>(null);

export function NavProvider({
  children,
  defaultExpanded = false,
}: {
  children: ReactNode;
  /** Read from the cookie on the server so the first paint is already correct. */
  defaultExpanded?: boolean;
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(defaultExpanded);

  const toggle = useCallback(() => setOpen((v) => !v), []);
  const close = useCallback(() => setOpen(false), []);

  const toggleExpanded = useCallback(() => {
    setExpanded((v) => {
      const next = !v;
      document.cookie = `${SIDEBAR_COOKIE}=${next ? "1" : "0"}; path=/; max-age=${COOKIE_MAX_AGE}; samesite=lax`;
      return next;
    });
  }, []);
